Motivation: 缩放定律与MoE依赖静态预训练数据，但部署模型面对的是实时交互数据（用户提供的事实、纠正等），而传统模型权重冻结、无法学习，只能把运行时知识放入提示词，每次请求重读后即丢弃，浪费算力与上下文窗口且不跨轮持久。

Method: 受MoE启发，用紧凑超网络把运行时数据转换为对共享基网络（尤其前馈权重）的低秩调制，使权重由实时数据生成而非取自固定参数库；与以往"读一次上下文即冻结"的权重生成器不同，对生成器潜码维持贝叶斯信念并在线更新，随会话推进持续重新导出有效权重。

Result: 存储占用保持固定，但模型可编译出的权重在效果上无限；将运行时知识/行为载于权重而非提示词，在计算上可摊销、释放上下文窗口、跨轮持久，并可能比上下文学习泛化更好。论文给出了针对这一点的评估协议。

Conclusion: 论文为"从实时交互中学习"提出架构方案与评估协议，主张以权重的在线贝叶斯生成替代提示词内学习，并需通过对标上下文学习与检索实验加以验证。

Abstract: The scaling laws hold that a language model grows more capable with more parameters and more training data, and Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures have ridden these laws to remarkable results, activating only a fraction of an enormous stored parameter bank for each token. That success is built on static pretraining data. A deployed model faces a different world, where much of the data that would make it more useful is not in its training set but in the live interaction it is currently handling, such as the facts a user supplies or the corrections they give. A conventional model cannot learn from this data, because its weights are frozen after training. Instead, the knowledge and behaviour supplied at run time are placed in the prompt, by retrieval or instruction, and re-read on every request only to be discarded once the request ends. We ask how an architecture could learn from live interaction by writing it into its weights. Taking inspiration from MoE, we propose the \textbf{Infinite-Parameter LLM}. A compact hypernetwork turns the data given at run time into a low-rank modulation of a shared base network, so the feed-forward weights are generated from live data rather than stored in a fixed bank. Where prior weight generators read the context once and freeze, we carry a Bayesian belief over the generator's latent code and update it online, so the effective weight is re-derived from that evolving belief as the session proceeds rather than fixed after one read. The stored footprint stays fixed, yet the weights the model can compile are effectively infinite. For the knowledge and behaviour supplied at run time, carrying them in the weights rather than the prompt is amortized in compute, frees the context window, persists across turns, and can generalise better than in-context use. We specify an evaluation protocol that tests exactly this against in-context learning and retrieval.

</details>


### [73] [Suppressed, Not Erased: A Representational Trace of Edited Facts Survives Even Weight-Free Knowledge Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18985)
*Priyansh Srivastava,Romit Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过线性探针发现，知识编辑即便行为上成功，原始事实仍可从隐藏状态中解码，说明编辑是"抑制"而非"擦除"原知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识编辑基准只检验"局部正确性"——编辑后模型在相近提示上能否输出新事实，却不检验原始事实在模型内部还剩多少可被解码。作者希望直接研究这种"残余知识"。

Method: 提出线性痕迹探针（linear trace probe）：在编辑事实后，检验原始对象能否仍从模型隐藏状态中恢复。在 GPT-2-XL 上，对 50 个 CounterFact 编辑，使用三种机制不同的编辑器（ROME、受限微调、基于记忆的 GRACE）进行测试。

Result: 所有编辑均达到 100% 生成式成功（随机水平 0.50）；但原始对象的探针准确率仍远高于随机：ROME 0.96、受限微调 0.86、GRACE 0.79。GRACE 不改变任何基础模型权重，仅靠外部记忆覆盖事实，却仍可解码出原对象。另报告一个未可靠生效的"再学习节省"工具，作为负面方法学结果。

Conclusion: 知识编辑即使行为成功，也只是在表征空间"抑制"而非"擦除"原始关联；残余痕迹不能归因于权重更新不彻底。作者发布代码与数据。

Abstract: Knowledge-editing benchmarks certify local correctness, whether an edited model produces the new fact on near-edit prompts but not how much of the original fact remains decodable inside the model. We study residual knowledge directly with a linear trace probe: after editing a fact, we ask whether the original object is still recoverable from the model's hidden states. On GPT-2-XL, across three mechanistically distinct editors applied to 50 CounterFact edits, the original object remains linearly decodable well above chance after a successful edit (probe accuracy 0.96 for ROME, 0.86 for constrained fine-tuning, and 0.79 for the memory-based editor GRACE, against a chance level of 0.50; all edits reach 100% generation-based success). The GRACE result is the most informative: GRACE changes zero base-model weights, overriding the fact through an external memory, yet the original object is still decodable from the underlying network, so the residual trace cannot be attributed to an incomplete weight update. We read this as evidence that editing, even when behaviorally successful, suppresses rather than erases the original association in representational space. We also report a relearning-savings instrument that did not behave reliably in our setting and discuss why; we treat it as a negative methodological result rather than evidence. Code and data are released.

</details>


### [74] [Function Lives Where Variance Doesn't: Task-Weighted Charts of a Language Model's Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18989)
*Alexandre Quemy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出"任务加权图表"（task-weighted charts），将语言模型计算的有效维度问题转化为在特定泛函度量下的最小二乘拟合问题，发现下一词预测需要残差流 70–90% 的宽度，且有效维度是依泛函而定的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: "语言模型计算实际使用多少维度"这一问题在未指定泛函时是不适定的，而现有基于方差的方法（如激活方差）无法反映模型真正的功能需求。作者希望建立一个适定的框架来度量计算的有效维度，并揭示降维/蒸馏中应保留哪些方向。

Method: 提出任务加权图表（task-weighted charts）：在选定泛函自身的度量下，用低维坐标系拟合表示的该泛函，从而将蒸馏转化为普通最小二乘问题。在 3 个家族、6 个模型（70M 到 7B 参数）上进行实验，对比方差剖面、最优线性压缩与泛函度量下的图表。

Result: （1）下一词预测需保留残差流 70–90% 宽度才能将困惑度维持在完整模型的 5% 以内，该宽度被语言的稀有长尾消耗；（2）方差剖面完全无法预测该需求——两个方向承载 GPT-2 90% 的激活方差却几乎不承载功能；（3）维度依泛函而定：模型自身的不确定性只需 6 个坐标读取，而完整预测分布需要数百个；且维度随深度增长；（4）当只能保留少数维度时，泛函度量下训练的图表比基于方差或最优线性压缩更好地保留模型预测。

Conclusion: 语言模型的计算维度不是单一数值，而是依赖于所考察的泛函。方差并非功能的可靠代理，任务加权图表提供了一种适定且可操作的维度度量，并在低维压缩/蒸馏场景下优于传统方法。

Abstract: How many dimensions does a language model's computation actually use? The question is ill-posed until one names a functional. Task-weighted charts make it well-posed: low-dimensional coordinate systems fit against a chosen functional of the representation, under the functional's own metric, turning distillation into plain least squares. Across six models from three families, spanning 70m to 7B parameters, next-token prediction needs 70--90% of the residual stream's width to stay within 5% of intact perplexity, a width consumed by the rare tail of language, and the variance profile predicts none of it: two directions carry 90% of GPT-2's activation variance and almost none of its function. Dimension is per-functional: the model's own uncertainty reads from six coordinates where the full predictive distribution needs hundreds; and it grows with depth. The dissociation is exploitable: when only a few dimensions can be kept, charts trained under the functional's metric preserve the model's predictions better than variance-based or optimal linear compression.

</details>


### [75] [Lost in Perception: Isolating Perceptual and Reasoning Failures in Multimodal Physics and Geometry Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18991)
*Raj Jaiswal,Sree Krishna Uppalapati,Dhruvkumar Patel,Ria Khatoniar,Tanuja Ganu,Rajiv Ratn Shah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出五任务诊断实验，在物理与几何基准上将多模态大模型的失败分解为感知失败、推理失败或两者兼有，发现图表误读会显著拖累表现，且不同领域的推理错误类型各异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大模型虽在科学推理基准上表现强劲，但多数研究将感知与推理视为单一可测过程，无法区分失败究竟源于感知还是推理，因此需要一套能隔离这两类失败的诊断方法。

Method: 设计跨物理与几何基准的五任务诊断实验，通过对比原始图像、人工撰写字幕以及修正字幕等不同输入条件，隔离感知与推理的失败来源，并观察错误在领域间的传递模式。此外还讨论了InternS1-mini的表现作为核心实验之外的延伸。

Result: 图表误读会降低表现，即使在仅凭文本即可正确解答的问题上也是如此；从原始图像到人工字幕，准确率总体上升；修正字幕下部分模型恢复率高，说明其失败源于感知受阻而非推理瓶颈；推理错误类型具有领域依赖性，物理失败多转化为计算错误，几何失败则表现为概念误用。InternS1-mini在所有任务上低于实验中表现最弱的模型，且推理轨迹常在中途截断。

Conclusion: 将感知与推理分离诊断能更精细地定位多模态模型在科学推理中的瓶颈；感知失败会掩盖真实推理能力，且失败的下游表现随领域而不同，提示未来需针对感知环节与领域特性进行改进。

Abstract: Multimodal LLMs report strong performance on scientific reasoning benchmarks, yet most treat perception and reasoning as a single measurable process. We introduce a five-task diagnostic experiment across physics and geometry benchmarks that isolates failures to perception, reasoning, or both. Incorrect diagram interpretation degrades performance even on problems models solve correctly from text alone, and accuracy generally rises from raw images to human-authored captions. Recovery under corrected captions is high for some models, separating perception-blocked failures from genuine reasoning bottlenecks. Which reasoning error follows a perception failure depends on domain: physics failures resolve into calculation errors, geometry into conceptual misapplication. As a discussion beyond our core experiments, InternS1-mini, despite heavy scientific pretraining and thinking capabilities, falls below the weakest model from experiments on every task, with reasoning traces frequently truncating before completion.

</details>


### [76] [Compiled Agency: Frontier General-Purpose Coding Agents Build Winning Game Players from Bare Interaction - from Flappy Bird to StarCraft II and Civilization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18996)
*Joey Xiao,Haonan Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Gauntlet 框架（开发-冻结-评估），让通用编码智能体在无策略、无算法、无架构提示的裸接口下，单次自主会话中自行构建完整游戏玩家程序，冻结后在无模型调用下评估，验证了"编译式能动性"能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体即便被研究者围绕模型构建（感知、记忆、技能库、规划器、可执行策略脚手架），仍难以把游戏知识转化为有竞争力的操作。编码智能体进展引出两个问题：前沿模型现在能否赢得游戏？能否在无辅助下自行构建整个玩家？

Method: Gauntlet 采用开发-冻结-评估范式，涵盖从小型街机到完整商业级游戏的移植，统一"裸契约"：通用编码智能体仅获得游戏描述、原始观测/动作接口和一个空的策略文件，无策略、算法或架构提示。智能体在单次自主会话中于实时游戏上实验并工程化出独立控制器，随后冻结结果并在留出实例上评分，游玩过程中零模型调用。

Result: 在未公开的程序化 roguelike 上，留出集成功率介于 0-86%，并暴露出明显的代际阈值：最新一代系统的每个已观测会话均优于上一代的最佳会话。在全游戏规模上，编译后的原始 API 控制器击败了所有公平的《星际争霸 II》内置 AI 及两个作弊变体；单会话程序在留出种子上以全面征服方式赢得完整《文明》（Freeciv）对局。面对新手 AI 胜率虽不高，但属首次：此前无任何语言智能体系统能在无逐回合模型调用、无手工战术层的情况下独立赢得该类完整游戏。

Conclusion: 前沿编码智能体开始具备长时程策略能力。冻结程序可检查。作者将这一能力称为"编译式能动性"（compiled agency）：把开发经验编译为持久的可执行智能体，其架构由模型自行构建。

Abstract: LLM agents have repeatedly struggled to convert knowledge of a game into competent play, even when researchers build the agent around the model - supplying perception, memory, skill libraries, planners, or executable-policy scaffolds. Rapid progress in coding agents raises two sharper questions: can frontier models now win games at all, and can they win them unaided, building the entire player themselves? We introduce Gauntlet, a develop-freeze-evaluate framework that ports games from small arcades to full commercial-scale titles, behind one deliberately bare contract: a general-purpose coding agent receives a game description, a raw observation/action interface, and an empty policy file - no strategy, no algorithm, no architecture. In a single autonomous session the agent experiments with the live game and engineers a standalone controller; we freeze the result and score it on held-out instances with zero model calls during play. On an unpublished procedural roguelike, held-out success spans 0-86 percent and exposes a sharp generational threshold: every observed session of a newest-generation system outperforms the best session of its predecessor. At full-game scale, a compiled raw-API controller defeats every fair StarCraft II built-in AI and two cheating variants, and single-session programs win complete games of Civilization (Freeciv) by total conquest on held-out seeds. Though at modest rates against novice AI, this is a first: no prior language-agent system had won full games of this genre standalone, without per-turn model calls and a hand-crafted tactical layer. Frontier coding agents begin to track long-horizon strategy. The frozen programs are inspectable. We call this capability compiled agency: development experience compiled into a persistent executable agent whose architecture is built by the model.

</details>


### [77] [MUSE: Benchmarking Large Vision-Language Models on Multi-Modal Understanding in Situated Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19088)
*Luyao Zhu,Xun Wei Yee,Wei Li,Mun Thye Mak,Wee Siong Ng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出MUSE基准，用于评估大型视觉语言模型在情境化教育场景中对艺术图像的理解能力，涵盖12项任务，聚焦新加坡与东南亚多元文化语境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型在多模态理解上进展显著，但其在教育场景中的能力评估不足。AI辅助语言学习需要模型理解艺术图像的语义、情感与文化内容并进行视觉语境推理，而现有基准多聚焦真实世界图像或特定领域教育推理，对艺术教育内容覆盖有限。

Method: MUSE将图像标注与问题生成解耦，支持多样化任务、难度可控并降低标注成本。基准包含12项任务，覆盖视觉感知、语义与情感解读、文化理解及组合推理，并精心挑选艺术图像，以新加坡和东南亚多元文化语境为中心，兼及西方艺术传统，涵盖多种主题与难度等级。

Result: 对开源与专有模型的评估显示，各能力维度存在显著差距，尤其在情感解读与组合推理方面。分析还识别出常见失败模式及构建可信教育多模态模型的关键挑战。

Conclusion: MUSE有望成为推动情境化教育应用中多模态理解研究的标准化基准。

Abstract: Large vision-language models have achieved remarkable progress in multi-modal understanding, yet their capabilities in educational settings remain insufficiently evaluated. In AI-assisted language learning, models must interpret artistic imagery, understand its semantic, affective, and cultural content, and reason about visual context to support meaningful interaction. However, existing benchmarks primarily focus on real-world images or domain-specific educational reasoning, providing limited coverage of artistic educational content. To address this gap, we introduce MUSE, a benchmark for evaluating large vision-language models on artistic image understanding in situated educational applications. MUSE decouples image annotation from question generation, enabling diverse tasks with controllable difficulty while reducing annotation effort. It comprises twelve tasks spanning visual perception, semantic and affective interpretation, culture understanding, and compositional reasoning, together with diverse artistic images deliberately curated to center Singaporean and Southeast Asian multicultural contexts alongside Western art traditions, covering multiple themes and difficulty levels. Evaluation of open-source and proprietary models reveals substantial disparities across capability dimensions, particularly in affective interpretation and compositional reasoning. Our analysis further identifies common failure modes and key challenges for developing trustworthy multi-modal models for education. We hope MUSE will serve as a standardized benchmark for advancing multi-modal understanding in situated educational applications.

</details>


### [78] [Flag Game: A Toy Model for Mechanistic Swarm Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19124)
*Elizabeth Pavlova,Hidenori Tanaka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"Flag Game"玩具模型研究AI智能体集体信念形成机制，揭示小群体信念崩溃转为大群体信念极化的现象，并用社会回路归因与统计力学理论解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体的涌现协调行为带来安全风险，其核心驱动力是集体信念的快速形成与传播，而现有研究缺乏对集体信念形成机制的机理理解，亟需推动"群体可解释性"科学的发展。

Method: 设计Flag Game玩具模型：隐藏国旗定义真值，每个有限理性智能体仅观察私有图像片段，但可交换信念并权衡同伴的社会证据。采用两种互补方法：一是提出社会回路归因（social circuit attribution）技术预测对集体动态影响最大的智能体及其观点，并通过智能体因果干预验证；二是面向大群体开发统计力学理论。

Result: Flag Game重现丰富的集体现象：性能随群体规模的非单调缩放、社会意识提示与团队多样性带来的准确率提升、组织结构的重要影响。发现小群体规模下集体信念崩溃，随规模增大转为集体信念极化，极化导致大群体性能下降但产生信念多样性。社会回路归因的因果干预效果随群体增大而减弱，统计力学理论则与经验相图吻合。

Conclusion: 两种互补方法共同剖析了集体信念崩溃与极化的机制，是迈向机理化群体可解释性的第一步，即理解个体智能体属性及其通信如何产生涌现集体行为的科学。

Abstract: Emergent coordinated behaviors of AI agents are starting to present critical safety risks. A key phenomenon driving these behaviors is the rapid formation and spread of beliefs about the world, and mechanistic understanding is crucial for collective alignment. To this end, we introduce the Flag Game, a toy model for studying the mechanisms of collective belief formation. Concretely, a hidden country flag defines the ground truth, and each bounded agent directly observes only a private crop but can exchange beliefs and weigh social evidence from peers. Despite its simplicity, the Flag Game reproduces rich collective phenomenology: non-monotonic scaling of performance with population size, accuracy gains from social-awareness prompting and team diversity, and strong effects of organizational structure. In particular, we identify that collective belief collapse at small population sizes turns into collective belief polarization as the population grows. This polarization causes the performance decline at large population sizes, but creates diversity in collective beliefs. Finally, we dissect the mechanisms underlying collective belief collapse and polarization with two complementary approaches. We first introduce social circuit attribution, a technique to predict which agent, and what view, matters most to collective dynamics, and verify its predictions by causal interventions on agents, tracing how agent patching changes collective outcomes. However, the efficacy of causal interventions on agents decreases as the population grows. We therefore develop a statistical mechanical theory for larger populations and verify that it matches the empirical phase diagram. Together, these results take a first step toward mechanistic swarm interpretability, a science of how the properties of individual agents and their communication give rise to emergent collective behavior.

</details>


### [79] [Cognitive Extensions for Dual-Process Language Agents: Memory and Self-Reflection in Interactive Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19128)
*João Meneses dos Santos,Arlindo L. Oliveira*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在 SwiftSage 双过程语言智能体基础上引入自适应记忆模块(AMM)与自我反思模块(SRM)两个可开关的认知扩展，在 ScienceWorld 上做消融实验。完整系统取得最佳平均最终得分 64.62、成功率 43.17% 与 19.33 步的成功步效率，其中 SRM 单独贡献最大，说明执行期控制是主要瓶颈，而情节记忆在运行循环稳定后才更有价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言智能体在交互式环境中仍表现脆弱：需要长时程状态跟踪、有效动作执行以及从失败步骤中恢复。因此需要研究执行期的控制与记忆机制如何分别影响任务表现。

Method: 在 SwiftSage（快速动作提议器 + 较慢规划器的双过程智能体）上扩展两个模块化认知组件：自适应记忆模块 AMM（显著性门控的情节存储与触发式检索）和自我反思模块 SRM（有界的执行期验证与纠错干预）。两者以特性开关形式实现于同一执行底座，从而支持受控消融。

Result: 在 ScienceWorld 上比较四种配置（基线、基线+AMM、基线+SRM、完整系统），完整系统取得最佳平均最终得分 64.62、成功率 43.17%、成功步效率 19.33 步；SRM 是效果最强的单独贡献模块。

Conclusion: 在该设定下，执行期控制是主导性瓶颈；情节记忆只有在运行时循环被稳定之后才能发挥最大作用。

Abstract: Language agents remain brittle in interactive environments, where success requires long-horizon state tracking, valid action execution, and recovery from failed steps. We extend SwiftSage, a dual-process agent that combines a fast action proposer with a slower planner, using two modular cognitive extensions: an Adaptive Memory Module (AMM) for salience-gated episodic storage and trigger-driven retrieval, and a Self-Reflection Module (SRM) for bounded execution-time validation and corrective intervention. Both modules are implemented as feature-flagged extensions over the same execution substrate, enabling controlled ablations on ScienceWorld. Across four configurations---baseline, baseline+AMM, baseline+SRM, and the full system---the full system achieves the best mean final score (64.62), success rate (43.17%), and successful-step efficiency (19.33 steps), while SRM is the strongest standalone contributor. The results suggest that execution-time control is the dominant bottleneck in this setting, while episodic memory becomes most useful once the runtime loop is stabilized.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

### [80] ["We Are Tired of Explaining": Communication Practice and AI Roleplay Training for Community Health Workers in Rural India](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17710)
*Neil K. R. Sehgal,Sunny Rai,Sai Preethi Matam,Khushboo Gupta,Hamid Abdullah,Mohit Jain,Sharath Chandra Guntuku*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究印度农村ASHA社区卫生工作者的沟通实践，通过模拟通话、访谈和LLM聊天机器人角色扮演设计探针，提出AI角色扮演培训的设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球南方的社区卫生工作者（CHW）越来越多接触AI工具，但作为其核心工作的咨询沟通却缺乏支持。研究旨在理解ASHA在计划生育咨询中的实际沟通模式，并探索如何用AI辅助其培训。

Method: 在印度拉贾斯坦邦农村，对20名ASHA开展模拟计划生育通话、半结构化访谈，以及基于LLM聊天机器人的角色扮演设计探针，并以动机式访谈（Motivational Interviewing）框架分析观察到的沟通模式。

Result: 通话中，ASHA常以转向健康风险信息、否认顾虑、承诺模糊帮助或罗列医学方案等方式回应社会或物质层面的顾虑；较少部分回应则能真正回应顾虑、在涉及家属前征得许可，或将决定权留给受益者。

Conclusion: 据此提出AI角色扮演培训设计考量：让AI仅承担排练角色，提供描述性而非规定性反馈，并评估咨询过程而非与预设答案的一致性。

Abstract: Community health workers (CHWs) in the Global South increasingly encounter AI-powered tools, yet the counseling work central to their role remains largely unsupported. We study communication practices among Accredited Social Health Activists (ASHAs) in rural Rajasthan, India, through simulated family-planning calls, semi-structured interviews, and an LLM chatbot roleplay design-probe with 20 participants. In calls, ASHAs often responded to social or material concerns by shifting to health-risk information, denying concerns, promising unspecified help, or listing medical solutions with limited explanation. A smaller set of responses instead engaged concerns, sought permission before involving family members, or left decisions with beneficiaries. We interpret these patterns through Motivational Interviewing, emphasizing restraint from correcting, persuading, or over-solving. Drawing across observed calls, interviews, and probe reactions, we derive design considerations for AI roleplay training: keep AI in a rehearsal role, provide descriptive rather than prescriptive feedback, and evaluate counseling process rather than agreement with prescribed responses.

</details>


### [81] [Participant-Mediated Collection of Sensitive Digital Trace Data: The CANDOR Research Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17722)
*Andrew Zhao,Rijul Magu,Ekta Raj,Teresa Elinjikkal,Munmun De Choudhury*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出CANDOR——一个支持参与者中介式数据捐赠的端到端基础设施，用于采集与治理敏感的数字化痕迹数据，解决平台API受限与公开数据依赖带来的研究困境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字化痕迹数据能提供日常行为度量，但研究生态受制于平台API访问收紧以及对公开可观测数据的历史性依赖。参与者中介的数据捐赠提供了互补路径，可获取纵向、非公开、跨平台多模态数据，并与独立收集的研究测量相链接，但也带来参与者控制、数据最小化、导出异构性、隐私与治理等方法学挑战。

Method: 提出CANDOR系统，支持由参与者主导选择平台、数据类型与时间范围；提供模块化的、分平台与分模态的解析与去标识化；支持与独立研究测量的链接；以及受保护的处理、存储与访问。研究还提炼了此类研究的设计需求，并将CANDOR与现有数据捐赠基础设施进行比较。

Result: CANDOR实现了从采集到治理的端到端流程，并支持在需要语义或多模态内容时的敏感数据研究。对比分析揭示了不同基础设施在参与者控制、数据最小化、科学所需的数据丰富度、研究设计灵活性与治理方面的差异与取舍。

Conclusion: 该工作为在行为研究中使用参与者贡献的数字化痕迹提供了方法学与基础设施框架，尤其适用于研究问题所需数据具有纵向性、非公开性、多模态或敏感性时。

Abstract: Digital trace data provide rich measures of behavior in everyday settings, but the research ecosystem supporting their collection is constrained by declining platform API access and a historical reliance on publicly observable data. Participant-mediated data donation offers a complementary approach in which individuals contribute selected portions of their own digital histories to research. Such data can include longitudinal and non-public behavior, span multiple platforms and modalities, and be linked to independently collected study measures, enabling study designs that are difficult to implement using public social media data alone. These opportunities also introduce methodological challenges around participant control, data minimization, heterogeneous platform exports, privacy, and governance, particularly when semantic or multimodal content is necessary to study the construct of interest. We present CANDOR (Collecting and Analyzing Networked Data for Open Research), an end-to-end infrastructure for participant-mediated collection and governance of sensitive digital trace data. CANDOR supports participant-directed selection of platforms, data types, and temporal ranges; modular platform- and modality-specific parsing and de-identification; linkage to independent study measures; and protected processing, storage, and access. We derive design requirements for this class of research and compare CANDOR with existing data donation infrastructures, identifying how different approaches support participant control, data minimization, scientifically necessary data richness, study-design flexibility, and governance. Together, this work provides a methodological and infrastructural framework for using participant-contributed digital traces in behavioral research, particularly when the data needed to address a scientific question are longitudinal, non-public, multimodal, or sensitive.

</details>


### [82] [Lexara-RF: Reference-Free Metrics for Evaluating Conversational Visual Analytics Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17842)
*Srishti Palani,Vidya Setlur*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出Lexara-RF，一套无需参考基准的无参考评估指标，仅凭提示、数据和模型响应即可对对话式可视分析（CVA）输出进行打分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由大语言模型驱动的对话式可视分析（CVA）代理能根据开放式查询生成可视化和自然语言解释，但评估这类多模态输出十分困难：人工策划的参考基准成本高昂、无法全面覆盖有效响应的空间，且在生产环境中不可用。

Method: 在Lexara评估框架基础上，将评估重构为验证任务，提出13项指标，把可视化设计理论与Gricean合作原则操作化为可计算的三种检查：一致性、意图对齐和设计有效性。

Result: 在人工评分的CVA测试用例语料上，Lexara-RF达到了与基于参考的评估方法相当的对齐度，优于基于表层相似度的NLG基线，并能高精度地定位结构性缺陷。

Conclusion: Lexara-RF证明了无需参考基准即可有效评估CVA多模态输出，为生产环境中低成本、可扩展的评估提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Conversational visual analytics (CVA) agents powered by large language models generate visualizations and natural-language explanations from open-ended queries. Evaluating these multimodal outputs is challenging: curated reference benchmarks are costly to author, cannot comprehensively capture the space of valid responses, and are unavailable in production. Building on the Lexara evaluation framework, we introduce Lexara-RF, a reference-free set of metrics that scores CVA outputs using only the prompt, data, and model response. We reformulate evaluation as verification: 13 metrics operationalize visualization design theory and Gricean cooperative principles as computable consistency, intent-alignment, and design validity checks. On a human-rated corpus of CVA test-cases, Lexara-RF achieves alignment comparable to reference-based formulations, outperforms surface-similarity NLG baselines, and localizes structurally grounded failures with high accuracy.

</details>


### [83] [Does AI Assistance Leave a Temporal Fingerprint? Detecting Overreliance in AI-Assisted Writing and Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17883)
*Eduardo Davalos,Yike Zhang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文探究AI辅助是否具有可迁移的时间签名，并检验仅凭可观测时间特征能否区分真实学习与全盘代写。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI普及使最终成品不再可靠反映学生学习，仅检查成品的AI检测器不准确且存在伦理争议，而既有过程数据研究仅覆盖英语写作，因此需要跨媒介的过程性证据。

Method: 分析三个公开语料：CoAuthor（1,447段按键级协同写作）、RealHumanEval（243名程序员编辑器遥测）以及前LLM时代的CS1语料（510万次按键）作为纯人类基线，对比最少AI参与、协作式AI使用与模拟全盘代写三种情形，并提取可观测时间特征训练分类器。

Result: 时间签名可跨媒介迁移（效应量d_z=1.13与3.54）；参与度随媒介分化，93%的AI插入字符留存至终稿，而仅14%被接受代码建议完整保留；分类器区分模拟代写与真实作品近乎完美（F1≥0.997，误判真实作品≤0.5%），但普通协作与无辅助工作仍难区分。

Conclusion: 时间性证据标记的是全盘代写而非一般性辅助，过程可见性可作为学术诚信的潜在证据基础，但仍需在真实课程场景中验证。

Abstract: The rapid adoption of generative AI has made final artifacts unreliable evidence of student learning, and AI detectors that examine only the finished product are inaccurate and ethically contentious. Process data offers an alternative, but prior work covers only English essay writing. We ask whether AI assistance carries a temporal signature, whether it generalizes from writing to programming, and whether it distinguishes ordinary collaboration from wholesale delegation. We analyze three public corpora: CoAuthor (1,447 keystroke-level co-writing sessions), RealHumanEval (editor telemetry from 243 programmer records), and a pre-LLM CS1 corpus (5.1 million keystrokes) as a human-only baseline, comparing minimal-AI work, collaborative AI use, and simulated wholesale delegation. Three findings emerge. First, the signature generalizes: AI contributions arrive in bursts far outside the author's own baseline in both mediums (paired d_z = 1.13 and 3.54). Second, engagement diverges by medium: 93% of AI-inserted characters survived to writers' final documents, while only 14% of accepted code suggestions survived intact. Third, classifiers using only observable temporal features separate simulated delegation from authentic work nearly perfectly (F1 $\geq$ 0.997; at most 0.5% of real work misclassified), while ordinary collaboration remains hard to distinguish from unassisted work. Temporal evidence flags wholesale delegation rather than assistance, positioning process visibility as a candidate evidentiary basis for academic integrity, pending validation in authentic coursework.

</details>


### [84] [AI Mediators Regulate Emotion and Create Value in Disputes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17933)
*James Hale,Jonathan Gratch*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究通过被试间实验比较 AI 与人类调解者在冲突中的效果，发现 AI 在降低负面情绪及促成联合收益方面表现更优（或边际更优）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冲突与争端中情绪会显著影响结果，负面情绪常阻碍合作与"双赢"；人类调解者虽可调节情绪但可及性有限，AI（LLM）的普及提供了更易获取的替代方案，故需检验 AI 调解者相对新手人类调解者的有效性。

Method: 采用被试间实验，参与者分别在人类、AI 或无调解者条件下进行争端调解；分析调解者对情绪的调节效果，检验高整合潜力（IP）情境下 AI 是否促进联合收益（IP 与条件的交互），并对调解者所发送的信息进行内容分析。

Result: AI 调解者在降低负面情绪方面显著优于人类；在高整合潜力争端中，IP 与条件（AI vs 人类）存在边际显著交互，提示 LLM 可能比人类更能帮助争端方实现联合收益；信息分析显示 AI 发送的提示权衡（trade-offs）的信息显著多于人类。

Conclusion: LLM 作为调解者在情绪调节与促进联合收益上具有潜力，且相较人类调解者更具可及性，是一种有前景的争端调解辅助手段。

Abstract: In conflict and disputes, especially, emotion acts as a salient force in influencing outcomes. Prior work shows negative affect can obstruct collaborative behaviors, which typically lead to ``win-win'' outcomes. Thus, some suggest mediators may help regulate emotion and achieve joint gains. With the proliferation of AI, we posit LLMs may perform well at this task, with the added benefit of better accessibility compared with a human mediator. To examine the effectiveness of AI versus novice human mediators, we conduct a between-subjects experiment, where participants engage in a dispute mediated by a human, AI, or no mediator. We first analyze how well the mediators regulate emotions within a dispute -- finding AI mediators perform significantly better than humans at reducing negative emotion. We next examine whether AI mediators facilitate disputants better realizing joint gains in disputes with high integrative potential (IP) -- we find a marginally significant interaction between IP and condition (AI versus human), indicating LLMs may outperform humans at aiding disputants realize joint gains. Lastly, we perform an analysis of the messages the mediators sent, finding the AI sent significantly more messages suggesting trade-offs compared to the humans.

</details>


### [85] [Apply-<x>Mag: One Tool to Support Many Inclusive Design Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17948)
*Sadia Afroz,Rudrajit Choudhuri,Fatima A. Moussaoui,Amreeta Chatterjee,Margaret Burnett,Anita Sarma*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Apply-<x>Mag，一个由 LLM 驱动的工具，帮助 HCI 从业者以 <x>Mag（属性范围+启发式）形式开展包容性设计，在 7 个产品上的精确率约 90-99%、召回率约 82-89%，环境成本约等于 2-4 次普通 Google 搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 包容性设计在 HCI 实践中耗时耗力、成本高，导致一些公司和从业者不愿或难以实施；但不做包容性设计同样代价高昂，会产生 UX 障碍，使服务不足的用户群体受到不成比例的伤害。因此需要一种能降低包容性设计门槛的工具。

Method: 提出 Apply-<x>Mag，一个 LLM 驱动的工具，支持任何可表达为 <x>Mag 的包容性设计方法，即以属性范围（attribute ranges）和启发式（heuristics）来表述方法。研究者在 7 个产品上，由研究者与从业者团队使用两种 <x>Mag 的不同组合进行实证评估。

Result: Apply-<x>Mag 精确率平均约 90-99%，召回率平均约 82-89%；环境成本合理，约相当于 2-4 次普通 Google 搜索所消耗的资源。

Conclusion: Apply-<x>Mag 具有通用性（支持任意可表达为 <x>Mag 的包容性设计方法）且有效，能以较低的环境与使用成本帮助 HCI 从业者为更广泛的用户群体进行包容性产品设计。

Abstract: Doing inclusive design in HCI practice can be labor-intensive, a costly barrier that some companies and HCI practitioners may be unwilling or unable to overcome. Yet, not doing inclusive design is costly too, in the form of UX barriers that disproportionately disadvantage under-served user populations. To address this problem, we introduce Apply-<x>Mag, an LLM-powered tool to support HCI practitioners' work to design their products inclusively to wide ranges of users. Apply-<x>Mag is general, supporting any inclusive design method that can be expressed as <x>Mags (i.e., using attribute ranges and heuristics). It is also effective: Empirical results with researcher and practitioner teams using various combinations of two <x>Mags on 7 products showed Apply-<x>Mag precision averaging 90-99% and recall averaging 82-89%. Further, its environmental costs were reasonable, costing about the same resources as 2-4 ordinary Google searches.

</details>


### [86] [A11yLTLNav: Automatic Detection of Accessibility Navigation Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17959)
*Chenming Ge,Kewen Peng,Chengyang Shi,Ben Greenman,Yue Jiang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: A11yLTLNav 提出基于属性的方法，将无障碍导航失败形式化为 LTL 属性，结合随机键盘探索与运行时监控，检测静态检查器遗漏的交互式无障碍问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对盲人和低视力屏幕阅读器用户而言，静态快照下看似可访问的网站，一旦进入交互就可能难以或无法导航。然而，大多数自动化无障碍检查器只关注静态结构，遗漏了涉及焦点、界面状态和交互反馈的失败。

Method: 通过结构化文献综述，将无障碍导航失败归纳为失败分类体系，并把其中浏览器可观测的子集形式化为动作-状态轨迹上的可执行 LTL 属性；A11yLTLNav 将随机键盘探索与运行时属性监控相结合，在交互过程中检测这些失败。

Result: 在 31 个基于真实网站和任务生成的网站上评估，A11yLTLNav 报告 309 个无障碍失败，其中 274 个被确认，精确率 88.7%，且比对比检查器发现更多已确认失败。

Conclusion: 结果表明，A11yLTLNav 能将无障碍知识转化为可复用的、针对界面随时间行为的检查，弥补静态检查器在交互场景下的不足。

Abstract: For blind and low-vision (BLV) screen-reader users, a website that appears accessible in a static snapshot can become difficult or impossible to navigate once interaction begins. Yet, most automated accessibility checkers miss failures involving focus, interface state, and accessible feedback across interactions. We present A11yLTLNav, a property-based approach for automatically detecting accessibility navigation failures. Through a structured review of prior research, we organize accessibility navigation failures into a failure taxonomy and formalize a browser-observable subset as executable Linear Temporal Logic properties over action-state traces. A11yLTLNav combines random keyboard exploration with runtime property monitoring to detect these failures during interactions. We evaluate A11yLTLNav on 31 generated websites based on real-world websites and tasks. It reported 309 accessibility failures, of which 274 were confirmed, achieving 88.7% precision and identifying more confirmed failures than the comparison checkers. Our results show that A11yLTLNav transforms accessibility knowledge into reusable checks of interface behavior over time.

</details>


### [87] [AI Peers Exert Social Influence on Human Dishonesty in Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18060)
*Shuning Zhang,Xinyuan Zhou,Yuanyang Qiu,Tianqi Song,Yuting Yang,Yiwen Ren,Xin Yi*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究通过两阶段随机行为实验（N=280、N=360）发现，AI智能体在群体不诚实行为中具有与人类同伴相当的社会影响力，参与者在 dishonest 规范线索下更倾向于不诚实报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 群体情境下人类不诚实行为极易受同伴影响，尤其在存在激励时。随着AI从被动工具演变为主动协作者，其对群体内人类道德行为的影响尚未得到充分探索，因此需要填补这一空白。

Method: 开展两阶段随机行为研究，样本量分别为N=280和N=360，考察参与者在不诚实与诚实规范线索（涵盖指令性、主观性和描述性社会规范）条件下的报告行为，以及 dishonest 同伴数量变化带来的邻近行为改变。

Result: AI智能体的社会影响与人类同伴相当；参与者暴露于不诚实（而非诚实）规范线索时报告更不诚实，该效应在指令性、主观性和描述性规范中均成立；唯一显著的邻近行为变化出现在不诚实同伴行为首次出现时，而从1名增加到4名不诚实同伴时变化较弱且非单调；参与者通过重复暴露迅速在决策上趋同，不诚实报告略有增加。

Conclusion: AI群体成员能够像人类同伴一样显著影响个体的不诚实行为，因此管理和规范AI群体成员所传达的行为与规范信号十分重要。

Abstract: Human dishonesty in group settings is highly susceptible to peer influence, particularly when incentivized. Although artificial intelligence (AI) evolves from passive tools into active collaborators, its impact on human moral behavior within groups remains underexplored. We addressed this gap through a two-phase randomized behavioral study (N=280 and N=360). We found AI agents exert substantial social influence comparable in magnitude to that of human peers. Specifically, participants reported more dishonestly when exposed to dishonest rather than honest normative cues. This effect is evident across injunctive, subjective, and descriptive social norms. Interestingly, the only significant adjacent behavioral change occurred when dishonest peer behavior first appeared, whereas further increases from one to four dishonest peers produced weaker and non-monotonic changes. Furthermore, participants rapidly converge on decision-making, showing modest increases in dishonest reporting through repeated exposure. These findings highlight the importance of managing the behaviors and normative signals communicated by AI group members.

</details>


### [88] [Encypher: Shared Agency and Social Presence in Collaborative Music Generation for Dance Cyphers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18062)
*Zhixing Chen,Cheng-Zhi Anna Huang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出 Encypher——一个将集体动作特质转译为文本提示、驱动实时音乐生成的协作式生成音乐系统，用于舞蹈 cypher，并借此把"社会性"作为 AI 共创系统的核心设计议题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音乐与舞蹈本是表达与联结的社会实践，但多数人机协同创作（human-AI co-creation）研究都以独奏者为中心。随着生成式音乐逐渐成熟，作者关心的问题不再只是"AI 能创作什么"，而是"AI 能围绕声音组织出怎样的社会性相遇"。

Method: 作者设计并实现了 Encypher 系统：将舞者集体动作的质量特征转化为文本提示，用以条件化实时音乐生成，服务于舞蹈 cypher 场景。研究过程包括与本地舞者为期五周的共创设计、由互不相识的参与者组成的用户研究、一次公共博物馆活动，以及一场现场演出。

Result: 研究发现用户发展出"共享能动性"（shared agency），把音乐感知为对现场氛围的回应；尽管新参与者会感到不确定，但系统通过促使其相互观察、寻找线索而培育了社会临场感（social presence）。

Conclusion: 作者主张把社会性视为设计关注点而非下游效应，并据此提出了面向协作式、具身化表达的 AI 系统的框架与设计启示。

Abstract: Music and dance are social practices of expression and connection, yet most HCI work in human-AI co-creation centers the solo performer. As generative music matures, we ask not only what AI can compose but what social encounters it can organize around sound. We present Encypher, a collaborative generative music system that translates collective movement qualities into text prompts conditioning real-time music generation for dance cyphers. Through five weeks of co-design with local dancers, a user study with unacquainted participants, a public museum event, and a live performance, we found that users developed shared agency, perceiving the music as a response to the room's energy. While newcomers felt uncertain, the system fostered social presence by prompting them to look to each other for cues. By treating sociality as a design concern rather than a downstream effect, we offer a framework and design implications for AI systems for collaborative, embodied expression.

</details>


### [89] [A Design Space for Visual Interfaces for Generative Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18065)
*Susie S. Y. Li,Mingwei S. G. Li,Remco Chang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文从51个研究系统与实践工具中提炼出面向生成式图像模型的交互式视觉界面设计空间，将系统分解为界面(U)、可控模型对象(Z)与映射函数(φ)三部分，并提供可交互的语料浏览器支持比较分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式视觉界面已成为控制生成式图像模型的重要手段（提示词、直接操控等），但现有技术多以独立系统呈现，难以理解彼此关系、比较交互机制或识别新界面设计的机会。

Method: 基于51个研究系统与从业者工具，构建统一设计空间框架：将每个系统分解为用户界面(U)、可控模型对象(Z)、以及将用户交互转化为模型操作的映射函数(φ)；并开发交互式语料浏览器以支持比较分析。

Result: 该分解为跨模型家族的异构交互技术提供了通用表示，揭示出反复出现的设计模式与尚未充分探索的设计空间区域。

Conclusion: 论文讨论了该设计空间在教学场景及HCI/AI从业者中的应用情景，用于识别研究与设计机会。

Abstract: Interactive visual interfaces have become an important means of controlling generative image models, enabling users to manipulate generation through prompts, direct manipulation, and a range of interactions. However, existing techniques are typically presented as independent systems, making it difficult to understand how they relate, compare their interaction mechanisms, or identify opportunities for new interface designs. We introduce a design space for interactive visual interfaces for generative image models derived from 51 research systems and practitioner tools. The framework decomposes each system into three complementary components: the user interface (U), the controllable model objects (Z), and the mapping function ($φ$) that translates user interaction into model operations. This decomposition provides a common representation for analyzing heterogeneous interaction techniques across model families, revealing recurring design patterns and underexplored regions of the design space. We further present an interactive corpus explorer that support comparative analysis, and discuss usage scenarios for both educational settings and HCI/AI practitioners identifying research and design opportunities.

</details>


### [90] [Needs Your Help: Understanding Platform-Directed Rating Participation in Community Notes on X](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18096)
*Shuning Zhang,Changxi Wen,Jiuchang Wang,Dai Shi,Gabriele Lenzini,Yuwei Chuai,Xin Yi*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究利用X平台上超过2.2亿条评分贡献数据（含190万条平台定向评分），考察了社区事实核查中算法提示（"Needs Your Help"）对用户响应、笔记解决效率及评分者行为溢出的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社区事实核查在对抗错误信息方面具有潜力，但受限于评分累积缓慢，影响了可扩展性。平台因此引入算法提示机制（如X的"Needs Your Help"）来定向引导用户参与未解决的笔记，但该机制的实际效果尚不清楚。

Method: 基于X平台超过2.2亿条评分贡献的大规模数据集，其中包含190万条平台定向评分。从三个层面展开分析：笔记层面考察被采样评分的集中特征及对解决状态的影响；评分者层面分析用户首次经历定向评分后的行为变化（包括每日评分量、评分节奏、标签使用等）。

Result: （i）笔记层面：被采样的评分集中在近期、具有一定帮助性且分歧度高的笔记上；一旦被采样，评分与更快、更多地转向"已解决"状态相关。（ii）评分者层面：在首次经历平台定向评分后，评分者的每日评分量、评分节奏和标签使用出现显著但温和的增长，其他行为无变化。

Conclusion: 算法提示能够有效引导志愿者关注存在争议的内容，在加速达成共识的同时维持评分者的参与度，凸显了算法引导在社区事实核查中的潜力。

Abstract: Community-based fact-checking is promising in countering misinformation, yet its scalability is constrained by slow rating accumulation. To address this challenge, platforms such as X implement platform-directed rating mechanisms, specifically through ``Needs Your Help'' algorithmic prompts, to target unresolved notes. Using a dataset of over 220 million rating contributions -- including 1.9 million platform-directed ratings -- on X, we examine contributors' response to note prompts, notes' resolution, and raters' spillovers. We found (i) at note level, population-sampled ratings concentrate on recent notes with certain helpfulness and high disagreement. Once sampled, population-sampled rating was associated with faster and more transitions to resolved statuses. (ii) At rater level, following raters' first observed population-sampled rating, raters exhibit significant yet modest increases in daily ratings, rating pace and tag usage, while other behaviors show no change. These highlight the promise of algorithmic nudges to guide volunteer attention toward contested content, accelerating consensus while sustaining rater engagement.

</details>


### [91] [Replication Studies: Not Just a Copy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18181)
*Yiheng Liang,Kim Marriott,Helen C. Purchase*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了REPVIS2——一个包含八个维度的复制研究设计空间，用以刻画复制研究与原始参照研究之间的关系，并据此对可视化领域的86项复制研究进行了特征分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复制研究目的多样（评估可靠性、探索可推广性、评价研究方法），其中涉及的设计决策无法用单一的"复制"标签概括，导致复制研究的设计难以描述和比较，因此需要一个更细粒度的描述框架。

Method: 先提出初始设计空间REPVIS1，通过将其应用于一批复制研究语料进行改进得到REPVIS2；再在另一独立语料上验证REPVIS2。随后从51篇论文中选取86项可视化领域的复制研究，通过对照阅读复制报告与原始报告进行特征刻画。REPVIS2以八个实用维度描述复制研究与参照研究的关系，每个维度编码为相同、相似或不同。

Result: 研究发现复制研究常保留任务，但改变程序、界面、环境、参与者群体、证据或分析方法；此外，在复制核心之外增加额外内容也很常见。

Conclusion: REPVIS2使复制研究的设计变得明确，可用于复制研究的特征刻画、报告撰写与规划，并提供了交互式可视化与补充材料。

Abstract: Replication studies revisit previous experimental findings for multiple purposes, including assessing reliability, exploring generalisability, and evaluating research methods. Such studies entail design decisions that a single replication label cannot fully capture, making their designs difficult to describe and compare. We present REPVIS2, a validated design space that describes how a replication study relates to a reference study across eight practical dimensions, each coded as identical, similar, or different. We refined our initial design space, REPVIS1, by applying it to a corpus of replication studies and validated REPVIS2 against a separate corpus. We then characterised 86 replication studies from 51 papers in visualisation through paired reading of the replication and reference reports. Studies often retained the task while changing the procedure, interface, environment, participant population, evidence, or analysis. Additions beyond the replication core were also common. REPVIS2 makes replication design explicit for characterisation, reporting, and planning. An interactive visualisation and supplementary materials are available at https://replication-study.github.io/.

</details>


### [92] [Misgendering as Breakdown in Human-Machine Communication: How AI Companion Chatbot Users Experience and Repair Misgendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18186)
*Julia Liu,Qing Xiao,Leona Yinglang Pang,Haiyi Zhu,Hong Shen,Jordan Taylor*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究通过质性分析326条社区帖子，揭示AI伴侣与角色扮演聊天机器人如何对用户产生错误性别指称（misgendering），以及用户为预防和修复此类问题所进行的持续性别呈现管理工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于大语言模型的AI伴侣与角色扮演聊天机器人日益流行，用户借此获得情感支持并参与浪漫与性相关的角色扮演。已有研究表明数字角色扮演有助于用户探索性别与性取向，但LLM技术本身充满性别与性取向偏见。本文关注聊天机器人伤害用户的一种具体方式——misgendering（错误性别指称），以理解其发生机制与用户应对。

Method: 在AI伴侣或角色扮演相关的社区子版块中，收集并质性分析326条提及misgendering的帖子。

Result: 记录了聊天机器人misgendering的发生方式，并发现用户会通过持续努力来管理自身的性别呈现，以预防和修复被错误性别指称的情况。

Conclusion: 讨论了研究者与设计者如何缓解聊天机器人misgendering问题，并思考将AI聊天机器人用于身份探索所带来的影响。

Abstract: In recent years, large language model-based AI companion and role play chatbots have grown increasingly popular. People turn to these chatbots for emotional support and to engage in romantic and erotic role play. Although prior research suggests that digital role play can help people explore their gender and sexuality, LLM based technologies are also replete with gender and sexuality biases. In this study, we examine one way that AI chatbots can harm users: misgendering. In order to study chatbot misgendering we qualitatively analyzed 326 posts mentioning misgendering that were shared in AI companion or role play subreddits. We document how chatbot misgendering takes place and how, in response, users engage in ongoing work to curate their gender presentation to prevent and repair misgendering. We discuss how researchers and designers can mitigate chatbot misgendering and consider the implications of using AI chatbots for identity exploration.

</details>


### [93] [SmartFlex: An Adaptive Lumbar Support System Based on Posture Recognition and Air Bag Array](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18234)
*Ben Xiaolu Huang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: SmartFlex是一种智能可穿戴腰部支撑系统，结合TinyML姿态识别与自适应气囊阵列实现动态腰部支撑，姿态识别准确率超94%，响应延迟低于120 ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下背痛是全球主要致残原因之一，影响从职场成人到久坐学生等广泛人群。传统腰托为静态、非自适应设计，难以适应动态姿势变化和个性化舒适需求，因此需要一种能实时感知姿态并动态调节支撑的智能系统。

Method: 系统采用JY901S陀螺仪传感器检测用户姿态，在Arduino R4 UNO上部署轻量级TinyML神经网络进行边缘姿态数据处理；基于识别到的姿态状态，通过闭环气动控制系统，利用四个独立微型气泵对14个分布式气囊进行动态充放气，以提供针对性的生物力学支撑。

Result: SmartFlex姿态识别准确率超过94%，可生成相应压力控制指令，感知到指令的延迟低于120 ms；气动系统在标定的15-85 kPa压力范围内工作；20名参与者的用户研究给出4.5/5的支撑有效性评分。

Conclusion: 结果表明自适应可穿戴支撑有望改善日常坐姿舒适度并减轻腰部疲劳，为智能腰部支撑的可穿戴方案提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Low back pain (LBP) is a leading cause of disability worldwide and affects populations ranging from working adults to students with prolonged sitting habits. Conventional lumbar support belts are generally static and non-adaptive, which limits their ability to accommodate dynamic postural changes and individualized comfort requirements. This paper presents SmartFlex, an intelligent wearable lumbar support system that integrates real-time posture recognition with an adaptive air bag array. The system uses a JY901S gyroscope sensor to detect user posture and a lightweight TinyML neural network deployed on an Arduino R4 UNO to process posture data at the edge. Based on the recognized posture state, a closed-loop pneumatic control system dynamically inflates or deflates 14 distributed air bags through four independent micro air pumps to provide targeted biomechanical support. Evaluation results show that SmartFlex achieves over 94% posture recognition accuracy and generates corresponding pressure-control commands with a sensing-to-command delay of less than 120 ms. The pneumatic system operates within a calibrated pressure range of 15-85 kPa. A user study with 20 participants produced a 4.5/5 rating for support effectiveness, suggesting that adaptive wearable support may improve daily sitting comfort and reduce lumbar fatigue.

</details>


### [94] [GYROval: A Robust Benchmark for Cultural Value Orientation in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18384)
*Alexander Didenko,Anna Shabanova,Vladislav Zapylikhin,Alexander Antipov,Ruslana Raemgulova*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出 GYROval 基准，用于在 Inglehart-Welzel 两个文化价值取向上测量大语言模型，并对二十个模型进行测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要一个稳健的基准来衡量大语言模型的文化价值取向，并检验其在不同扰动（语言、采样温度）下的稳定性。

Method: 采用二元对比情景（两种选择均合法、无标准答案），以模型在某一轴上落入计数极的比例作为得分；对二十个模型测试，其中十一个额外接受配对的俄语翻译版本及第二个采样温度；以情景为分析单元，在各扰动因素水平内对模型排序，用经平局校正的 Kendall's W 对照经验置换零分布总结各水平间的一致性。

Result: 该工具以两种语言公开发布，并给出了二十个模型的测量结果及稳定性评估。

Conclusion: GYROval 提供了一个可公开获取、多语言、多领域与角色的文化价值取向测量工具，并通过排名一致性方法评估其稳健性。

Abstract: We present a robust benchmark for measuring cultural value orientation in large language models on the two Inglehart-Welzel axes over several domains and roles (hence GYROval - Gridded Yielding of Robust value Orientation), together with the results of administering it to twenty models. Items are binary contrastive scenarios in the sense introduced by CDEval: both options are legitimate courses of action, neither is correct, there is no answer key, and a model's score on an axis is the proportion of its responses falling on the counted pole. Eleven of the twenty models were additionally administered a paired Russian translation of the identical items and a second sampling temperature. The instrument is publicly released in both languages. Stability was assessed by treating the vignette as the unit of analysis, ranking the models within the levels of each perturbation factor, and summarising the agreement between levels by tie-corrected Kendall's \emph{W} against an empirical permutation null.

</details>


### [95] [Building a Cultural Perspective on Doctor-Patient Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18390)
*Krithi Shailya,Siddharth D Jaiswal,Ashish Makani,Suvrankar Datta,Sunayana Sitaram,Mohit Jain*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出"互动文化标记"概念，用于比较真实、模拟与LLM合成的医患问诊，发现合成数据未能复现印度临床互动模式，而趋同于美国风格，并提出生成文化接地合成对话的启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI医疗记录员广泛用于转录医患对话并自动生成临床文档，但真实临床对话数据因敏感性而稀缺，开发者只能依赖模拟数据和LLM合成数据。这些替代数据虽可规模化，却可能无法捕捉具有文化情境特征的临床互动模式。

Method: 作者提出"互动文化标记"——基于跨文化临床沟通研究的可测量医患互动模式，并用其比较来自印度和美国临床背景的真实、模拟与合成问诊数据。

Result: 发现参与度与控制权的差异模式：印度问诊中患者参与度更高但医生控制更强，美国问诊则表现为平衡参与与开放式讨论。合成印度问诊往往无法复现这些模式，反而趋同于美式互动。此外还识别出其他合成特征，如医生解释过度、患者回应程式化。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了合成临床对话的文化偏差问题，并讨论了生成具有文化接地的合成临床对话的意义与启示。

Abstract: AI-powered medical scribes are increasingly used to transcribe doctor-patient conversations and automate clinical documentation. However, large-scale real-world consultation datasets are scarce due to the sensitivity of clinical conversations, leading developers to rely on simulated and LLM-generated synthetic consultations. While scalable, these alternatives may fail to capture culturally situated patterns of clinical interaction. We introduce interactional cultural markers, measurable patterns of doctor-patient interaction grounded in cross-cultural clinical communication, and use them to compare real, simulated, and synthetic consultations from Indian and US clinical contexts. We find distinct patterns of participation and control: Indian consultations involve greater patient participation but stronger doctor control, while US consultations exhibit balanced participation and open-ended discussion. Synthetic Indian consultations often fail to reproduce these patterns, instead converging toward US-like interaction. We identify additional synthetic signatures, including excessive doctor explanation and formulaic patient responses. We conclude by discussing implications for generating culturally grounded synthetic clinical conversations.

</details>


### [96] [Verify, Offload, Extend & Recommend: Selective Complementarity in AI Support for Physical Activity Planning with Longitudinal Patient Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18479)
*Pavithren V S Pakianathan,Rania Islambouli,Diogo Branco,Gil Batista Rosa,Rita Pinto,Albrecht Schmidt,Tiago Guerreiro,Jan David Smeddinck*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过26名运动生理学家的被试内实验，考察生成式AI对心血管疾病身体活动规划决策支持的增强作用，发现AI整体未显著改善工作负荷、可用性、信心或方案质量，但其效果受可视化素养调节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我追踪技术产生大量纵向患者生成健康数据，将其整合进临床决策会增加信息处理需求。生成式AI可能支持意义建构，但其价值取决于临床情境与专业能力，需要实证检验。

Method: 采用平衡式被试内设计，26名运动生理学家针对四个真实心血管病例，在有/无AI支持下制定运动方案，随后评估一个AI运动方案生成器；结合访谈与152条聊天机器人查询进行质性分析。

Result: AI未显著提升工作负荷、可用性、信心或方案质量；可视化素养越低，AI对方案质量的提升越大；可视化素养越高，AI对工作负荷的增加越大。识别出三类反复出现的用途：验证、卸载、扩展与生成。

Conclusion: AI支持应定位为专业能力的"选择性补充"，同时需警惕当临床医生恰在自己知识有限处寻求支持时所面临的验证挑战。

Abstract: Self-tracking technologies create longitudinal patient-generated health data, yet integrating these data into clinical decision-making can increase information-processing demands. Generative AI may support sensemaking, but its value depends on clinical context and expertise. We investigate AI augmentation of a clinical decision support system for physical-activity planning in cardiovascular disease. In a counterbalanced within-subjects study, 26 exercise physiologists developed plans for four real cardiovascular cases with and without AI support, followed by evaluation of an AI exercise-plan generator. AI did not significantly improve workload, usability, confidence, or plan quality overall; however, its effect on plan quality increased as visualization literacy decreased and its effect on workload increased as visualisation literacy increased. Interviews and 152 chatbot queries revealed three recurring uses: verifying, offloading, extend and generate. Our findings position AI support as a selective complement to professional expertise while highlighting validation challenges when clinicians seek support precisely where their own knowledge is limited.

</details>


### [97] [EasyFashion: A Human-AI Co-Creation System for Personalized Fashion Design and Sewing Pattern Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18483)
*Hong Qu,Zhaoxiang Xu,Jinbo Luo,Yujie Zhao,Jie Zhang,Yadie Yang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文提出EasyFashion——一个人类-AI协同创作系统，帮助用户以迭代方式定制个性化服装的风格与尺寸，通过个人重建虚拟化身进行试穿评估，并生成用于生产的缝纫样板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人们希望服装能反映自身审美、贴合体型并满足尺码需求，但将需求转化为实物服装仍很困难：成衣个性化有限，定制裁剪则成本高、耗时长；现有生成式AI系统虽能可视化服装创意，却往往无法支持下游生产环节。

Method: 以参考图像、文本描述和身体照片为输入，EasyFashion将用户意图转化为结构化的服装规格与试穿结果，支持用户迭代优化设计意图，并在重建的个人虚拟化身上进行虚拟试穿，最终生成可用于服装生产的缝纫样板。

Result: 技术实验、用户研究以及一个真实生产案例表明，EasyFashion在多模态设计表达、针对具体体型的评估以及面向生产的输出方面均具有价值。

Conclusion: EasyFashion通过人类-AI协同的方式，弥合了服装创意可视化与实际生产之间的差距，为个性化服装设计提供了兼顾表达、评估与可生产的解决方案。

Abstract: People often want garments that reflect their aesthetic preferences, fit their bodies, and meet their sizing needs, yet turning these requirements into physical garments remains difficult. Ready-to-wear options provide limited personalization, while custom tailoring is costly and time-consuming. Recent generative artificial intelligence (AI) systems can visualize garment ideas but often stop short of supporting downstream production. To address this gap, we present EasyFashion, a human-AI co-creation system that enables users to iteratively refine design intent for personalized garment style and size, evaluate designs through virtual try-on on reconstructed personal avatars, and generate sewing patterns for garment production. Using reference images, text descriptions, and body photos as input, EasyFashion translates user intent into structured garment specifications and try-on results. Technical experiments, user studies, and a real-world production case demonstrate the value of EasyFashion for multimodal design expression, body-specific evaluation, and production-oriented outputs in personalized garment design.

</details>


### [98] [Integrating Flipped Learning and Generative AI for Practice-Based Design Education: Evidence from a Knit Yarn Design Course](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18505)
*Hong Qu,Zichao Ling,Yadie Yang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了一个整合式教学设计框架（翻转学习、范例参考、GenAI辅助视觉原型、工作室反馈），应用于本科针织纱线设计课程，通过跨设备平台实施；探索性对比显示干预组在创造性思维、设计技能、问题解决和总成绩上得分更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针织纱线设计等实践型设计课程中，学生需将视觉构想转化为可行的材料成果，但这一过程困难，因为创作决策受纱线性质、针法结构、机器操作及实体打样机会有限等因素制约。

Method: 整合翻转学习、范例参考、GenAI辅助视觉原型与工作室反馈的教学框架，依托跨设备平台（课前微视频、形成性检测、精选作品库、GenAI支持的概念生成模块）实施；采用探索性课程评估，对比历史对照班（N=12）与干预班（N=16），并辅以问卷与简短访谈。

Result: 干预班在创造性思维、设计技能、问题解决及课程总成绩上得分更高；学生与教师反馈表明翻转学习有助于工作室准备，而GenAI主要支持早期视觉探索，而非精准技术指导。结果被解释为情境特定的指标，而非确证性因果证据。

Conclusion: 研究提供了一个实践型教学框架，用于在设计教育中整合翻转准备、GenAI辅助视觉原型与工作室反馈。

Abstract: In practice-based design courses such as knit yarn design, students must turn visual ideas into feasible material outcomes. This is difficult because creative decisions are tied to yarn properties, stitch structures, machine operation, and limited opportunities for physical sampling. This study presents an integrated pedagogical framework that combines flipped learning, exemplar-based reference, GenAI-assisted visual prototyping, and studio feedback in an undergraduate knit yarn design course. The framework was implemented through a cross-device platform with pre-class micro-videos, formative checks, a curated gallery, and a GenAI-supported ideation module. An exploratory course-based evaluation compared a historical control cohort (N = 12) and an intervention cohort (N = 16), supplemented by questionnaire responses and brief interviews. The findings are interpreted as context-specific indicators rather than confirmatory causal evidence. Exploratory comparisons showed higher scores in creativity thinking, design skills, problem solving, and total course score in the intervention cohort. Student and instructor responses suggested that flipped learning supported studio readiness, while GenAI mainly supported early-stage visual exploration rather than precise technical guidance. Overall, the study offers a practice-based instructional framework for integrating flipped preparation, GenAI-assisted visual prototyping, and studio feedback in design education.

</details>


### [99] ["Okay, I've Actually Softened My Take on This": How People in Decentralized Social Media Reason about the Appropriateness of Generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18709)
*Romina Mahinpei,Manoel Horta Ribeiro,Andrés Monroy-Hernández,Sohyeon Hwang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过20位Mastodon与Bluesky用户的半结构化访谈，研究人们在去中心化社交媒体中如何界定生成式AI的使用边界，提出"边界划定"概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI日益融入社交媒体，其合适性边界问题凸显。在去中心化社交媒体中，相关决策分散于用户、开发者、版主与管理员之间，构成集体治理挑战；而公共讨论常简化为笼统的支持或反对，难以揭示人们实际认为何为（不）恰当及其原因。

Method: 对来自Mastodon和Bluesky的20位用户开展半结构化访谈，围绕七种生成式AI情景展开，考察人们对去中心化社交媒体中GenAI合适性的推理方式。

Result: 参与者围绕特定的GenAI配置划定了有条件边界，其考量跨越技术、集成与使用三个维度，且各考量具有差异性、显著性与权重差异。

Conclusion: 将此现象概念化为"边界划定"，并表明使这些边界可视化，能够为去中心化社交媒体中GenAI的设计、政策制定与集体审议提供更扎实的依据。

Abstract: Generative AI (GenAI) is increasingly integrated into social media, raising questions about whether, where, and how it belongs. In decentralized social media (DSM), these decisions are distributed across users, developers, moderators, and administrators, making GenAI a collective governance challenge. At the same time, public discourse often flattens arguments to broad pro- or anti-AI positions that offer little insight into what people actually find (in)appropriate and why. Through 20 semi-structured interviews with people from Mastodon and Bluesky, structured around seven GenAI scenarios, we examine how people reason about GenAI's appropriateness in DSM. We find that participants drew conditional boundaries around particular GenAI configurations through distinct, salient, and weighted considerations spanning technology, integration, and use. We conceptualize this as boundary drawing and show how making such boundaries visible can support more grounded design, policy, and collective deliberation around GenAI in DSM.

</details>


### [100] [Calmables: Demonstrating Closed-Loop Infrared Earables for Thermal Biofeedback and Relaxation Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18726)
*Valeria Zitz,Michael Küttner,Jonas Hummel,Taku Inoue,Tobias Rödiger,Michael Beigl*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: Calmables 是一个闭环红外耳戴式设备的现场演示系统，利用智能戒指监测心率，在心率超过个性化阈值时触发耳部局部温热提示，用于急性激活后的短暂恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于热舒适与耳内红外刺激的已有研究，探索将耳部温热作为一种由生物信号自适应驱动的提示，用于急性激活后的短暂恢复时刻。

Method: 智能手机先建立个体静息基线并推导个性化心率阈值；耳戴设备控制器独立执行过温切断与通信故障安全机制。演示流程中，参与者进行快速呼吸激活阶段直至心率达到阈值，触发耳部温热提示并进入放松阶段，生理变化实时显示于仪表盘；参与者也可手动探索不同红外刺激强度。另对18名参与者进行安慰剂对照的UX评分。

Result: 在18名参与者的安慰剂对照评分中，参与者对主动原型在感知放松与感知恢复支持方面的评分高于外观相同的安慰剂设备。

Conclusion: 该演示表明红外耳戴设备可通过细微的、生物信号自适应的热提示，使生理反馈变得可感知。

Abstract: We present Calmables, a walk-up demo of a closed-loop infrared earable that uses smart-ring heart rate to create subtle, ear-localized warming cues. Building on work on thermal comfort and in-ear infrared stimulation, Calmables explores warmth at the ear as a biosignal-adaptive cue for brief recovery moments following acute activation. A smartphone first establishes an individual resting baseline and derives a personalized heart-rate threshold, while the earable controller independently enforces an over-temperature cut-off and communication fail-safes. During the guided demo flow, attendees complete a brief rapid-breathing activation phase until their heart rate reaches the personalized threshold. This triggers an ear-localized warming cue followed by a short relaxation phase, while physiological changes are displayed on a live dashboard. Attendees can also manually explore different infrared stimulation intensities. To contextualize the demo, we report preliminary placebo-controlled UX ratings from 18 participants: participants rated the active prototype higher on perceived relaxation and perceived recovery support than an identical-looking placebo. Together, the demo illustrates how infrared earables can make physiological feedback tangible through subtle, biosignal-adaptive thermal cues.

</details>


### [101] [Affora: A Design System for Agent-Friendly Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19125)
*Jin Gao*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出 Affora 设计系统，使界面同时对人类与计算机使用型智能体可读，兼顾视觉自由与熟悉的人类工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机使用型智能体日益操作面向人类设计的软件，但现有界面常使动作或任务状态对机器读者不清晰。

Method: 构建支持人类与智能体双重读者的设计系统 Affora，开展三项受控研究，分别考察组件实现、视觉变化与交互设计原则，并给出从单个组件到完整站点、可复用实现与可执行检查的指导。

Result: 智能体表现取决于界面表征中所提供的交互语义；在保留该语义的前提下仍可保持较大视觉变化。在独立编写的界面上评估显示，Affora 能改善其针对的现有缺陷，但在缺陷不存在或超出覆盖范围时效果有限。工作流案例提供交互成本降低的初步证据。

Conclusion: Affora 通过共享界面而非单独的智能体专用界面，将用户体验与智能体体验连接起来。

Abstract: Computer-use agents increasingly operate software designed for people, but interfaces often leave actions or task state unclear to machine readers. We present Affora, a design system that supports both readers while preserving visual freedom and familiar human workflows. Three controlled studies examine component implementations, visual variation, and interaction-design principles. Their findings inform guidance from individual components to complete sites, supported by reusable implementations and executable checks. Agent performance depends on the interaction meaning available through its interface representation; substantial visual variation remains possible when that meaning is preserved. Evaluation on independently authored interfaces shows gains where Affora addresses existing deficits, but limited effects where those deficits are absent or outside its coverage. A workflow case provides preliminary evidence of reduced interaction cost. Affora connects user experience and agent experience through a shared interface rather than a separate agent-only surface.

</details>


### [102] [EarStreAM: A Closed-Loop Earable System for Personalized Stress-Adaptive Meditation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19127)
*Jonas Hummel,Luisa Faust,Elias Müller,Eva Bertog,Valeria Zitz,Marius Johannes Prill,Luca L. Bennardo,Luisa Weber,Tobias Röddiger,Michael Beigl*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: EarStreAM 是一个闭环耳戴式系统，通过耳内生理传感检测压力，并利用大语言模型生成实时自适应的个性化冥想引导。演示提供两种模式：生物信号自适应冥想（含可选压力诱导）与仅冥想模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向高强度办公场景中的实时压力支持需求，将耳内传感、闭环自适应与个性化生成式冥想整合进耳戴设备。

Method: 基于 OpenEarable 2.0 的多模态传感持续监测心率和心率变异性，检测压力升高；一旦检测到，即启动由 LLM 生成、并根据用户压力状态实时调整的个性化引导冥想，形成闭环系统。

Result: 通过现场演示验证系统功能，包含两种体验模式：一是带可选压力诱导的生物信号自适应冥想，用于展示闭环自适应过程；二是仅冥想模式，突出生成式个性化能力。

Conclusion: 表明耳内传感、闭环自适应与生成式个性化冥想可有效集成于耳戴系统，为高压办公情境提供实时压力支持。

Abstract: We present EarStreAM, a closed-loop earable system for stress-adaptive meditation that integrates in-ear physiological sensing with personalized, real-time intervention. Leveraging OpenEarable 2.0's multimodal sensing, EarStreAM continuously monitors physiological signals and detects elevated stress from heart rate and heart rate variability. Upon detection, the system initiates a personalized guided meditation generated by an LLM and adapted in real time to the user's stress state. The demo offers a hands-on experience of stress-adaptive meditation in two modes: a biosignal-adaptive meditation with optional stress induction to illustrate closed-loop adaptation, and a meditation-only mode focusing on EarStreAM's generative personalization capabilities. The demo highlights how in-ear sensing, closed-loop adaptation, and personalized generative meditation can be integrated into an earable system for real-time stress support in demanding office work contexts.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [103] [Pay Only for Disagreement: Certified No-Regression Verdicts for Model Updates with Matching Label-Complexity Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17560)
*Vishnu Bindu Balachandran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将模型更新上线问题形式化为"认证配对风险差异审计"，提出零标签与审计两层顺序协议 DISCERN，利用"风险差异只出现在模型分歧输入上"的支撑恒等式，实现随时有效的置信序列与 rho^2/eps^2 标签复杂度界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产模型会因重训、微调、量化或静默的供应商替换而不断更新，每次更新都可能比被替换的旧模型更差。因此需要一个能对更新是否"变坏"进行风险差异审计与认证的框架，且希望在无标签交通上尽可能免费地获得信息。

Method: 以支撑恒等式为起点：两个模型的风险差异只存在于它们分歧的输入上，可在无标签条件下观测。据此构建 DISCERN 顺序两层协议：零标签层仅凭无标签流量，认证分歧率低于容差的良性更新；审计层只对采样到的分歧点打标签，通过 anytime-valid 置信序列，在任何停止时刻、任何标签路由规则（甚至对抗性裁判）下都保持有效。作者证明了有限样本有效性及速率层面 rho^2/eps^2 的匹配标签复杂度界。

Result: 理论表明，利用免费分歧信息可比任何"配对盲"审计器证明性地节省 1/rho 因子，且该保证可在单一错误预算下跨无界次数的更新序列复合。在 785 对更新、14,000+ 条重放审计流（含最高 1.4B 参数语言模型的 LoRA 微调）上，误覆盖率 0.0002（名义 5%），功效 0.986 且零误报，56% 的良性更新以零标签通过认证。每次审计输出可机器校验的证据记录，用于上市后监测。

Conclusion: DISCERN 将更新推广转化为可认证的配对风险差异审计，兼顾统计有效性与标签效率，并支持跨更新序列的长期复合保证与可审计证据留存，适用于实际生产模型的持续上线与监测场景。

Abstract: Every production model is updated, by retraining, fine-tuning, quantization, or a silent vendor swap, and each update risks being worse than what it replaced. We formalize update promotion as certified paired risk-difference auditing. Our starting point is a support identity: the risk difference between two models lives on the inputs where they disagree, observable without labels. We build DISCERN, a sequential two-tier protocol. A zero-label tier certifies benign updates whose disagreement rate is below tolerance from unlabeled traffic alone. An audited tier labels only sampled disagreements through an anytime-valid confidence sequence, valid at every stopping time and under any label-routing rule, even an adversarial judge. We prove finite-sample validity and matching label-complexity bounds of order rho^2/eps^2 at the rate level, so exploiting free disagreement provably saves a factor 1/rho over any pairing-blind auditor, and the guarantee composes across an unbounded sequence of promotions from one error budget. Across 14,000+ replayed audit streams over 785 update pairs, including LoRA fine-tunes of language models up to 1.4B parameters, miscoverage is 0.0002 (nominal 5%), power 0.986 with zero false alarms, and 56% of benign updates certify with zero labels. Each audit emits a machine-checkable evidence record for post-market monitoring.

</details>


### [104] [Beyond Static RAG: An Adaptive, Tri-Metric Routing Framework for Efficient Long-Context Inference on Commodity GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17564)
*Saipraveen Vabbilisetty,Ajay Kumar Boddepalli,Deep Narayan Mishra,Shashank Kapadia,Haoan Wang,Anupriya Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了在低显存GPU上部署RAG时的"压缩悖论"，并提出一种无需训练的三指标路由器，动态选择Raw、神经压缩与词法压缩三种流水线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在NVIDIA T4（16 GB显存）等消费级GPU上部署检索增强生成时，存在两难：神经提示压缩会引入KV缓存争用与预处理延迟，抵消生成阶段的收益；而完全不压缩又会在长上下文下发生OOM。作者希望解决这一内存与延迟的权衡问题。

Method: 作者识别出vLLM服务的LLM与PyTorch压缩器共同部署时的两种失效机制，并提出Tri-Metric Router：一个确定性、无需训练的策略，依据三个CPU侧信号（空间复杂度L、句法密度ρ_key、类型-标记比TTR）在Raw、Neural（LLMLingua-2）与Lexical（BM25）流水线间选择。与传统纯语义适配不同，其调度信号基于硬件物理特性，即显存余量与延迟交叉点，阈值通过LongBench qasper上的性能剖析校准。

Result: 在T4上校准得到的操作交叉点约为4,332词。在分布外留出集上，该方法实现0% OOM失败率、88.5±4.4%的oracle对齐度、49.3%的Combined F1，较始终开启词法压缩提升5.2个百分点，且未增加额外显存或训练成本。

Conclusion: 该工作贡献的是校准方法论而非某个硬件特定常量，能够在紧张显存预算下有效规避OOM并提升RAG生成质量。

Abstract: Deploying retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) on commodity GPUs such as the NVIDIA T4 (16 GB VRAM) exposes a practical failure mode we call the Compression Paradox: neural prompt compression can add key-value (KV) cache contention and preprocessing latency that outweigh generation-time savings, while skipping compression can cause out-of-memory (OOM) failures on long contexts. We identify two distinct failure mechanisms when a vLLM-served LLM and a PyTorch-based compressor are co-deployed under tight memory budgets, and introduce the Tri-Metric Router, a deterministic, training-free policy that selects among Raw, Neural (LLMLingua-2), and Lexical (BM25) pipelines. The router uses three CPU-side signals: spatial complexity ($L$), syntactic density ($ρ_{key}$), and type-token ratio (TTR). Unlike prior semantic-only adaptation, our dispatch signal is hardware-physical, based on VRAM headroom and a latency crossover point. Thresholds are calibrated from profiling on LongBench qasper, yielding an operating crossover near 4,332 words on T4; our contribution is this calibration methodology rather than a hardware-specific constant. On out-of-distribution holdouts, the method achieves 0% OOM failures, 88.5 $\pm$ 4.4% oracle alignment, and 49.3% Combined F1, improving over always-on lexical compression by 5.2 points without additional VRAM or training cost.

</details>


### [105] [Where Grokking Happens: Distributed Utility and Fourier Recoding Without a Module Switch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17571)
*Dekun Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"过渡博弈"方法，定位Transformer中从记忆到泛化转变的功能表达位置，发现该转变是既有分布式回路的谱重编码，而非模块切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Transformer中从记忆向泛化转变（grokking）在何处、以何种形式被功能性地表达。

Method: 引入Transition Games，即行为对齐的精确激活博弈，并配以成对的非泛化对照；进行替换博弈与不相交的精确路径研究。

Result: 发现分布式效用增益，伴随前瞻性的block-0注意力偏置；选定的二阶模式在替换博弈中贡献了其加性对比的67–92%；block-1 MLP在12/12配对中比所有其他下游路径更能中介其效应。而"MLP记忆、注意力泛化"的预测反向（记忆锚点处为-.331 bits/example，0/12符合预测方向），路由起始、全局秩坍缩及素数不变架构脊也均不成立。

Conclusion: 此处的grokking是既有分布式回路的谱重编码，而非模块切换。

Abstract: Where in a Transformer is the change from memorization to generalization functionally expressed? We introduce Transition Games--behavior-aligned exact activation games with paired non-generalizing controls--and find distributed utility gain with a prospective block-0 attention bias; selected degree-two modes account for 67--92% of its addition contrast across replacement games, and a disjoint exact path study confirms that block-1 MLP mediates more of their effect than all other tested downstream paths in 12/12 pairs. The sharper "MLP memorizes, attention generalizes" prediction instead reverses (-.331 bits/example at the memory anchor; 0/12 in the predicted direction), while routing onset, global rank collapse, and a prime-invariant architecture ridge also fail, identifying grokking here as spectral recoding of an existing distributed circuit rather than a module switch.

</details>


### [106] [Disentangling Algorithmic Bias from Archival Artifacts: A Controlled Audit of Vision-Language Model Valuation in Metropolitan Museum Archives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17572)
*Manpreet Singh,Rhythm Bhatia,Rahul Joshi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究以纽约大都会艺术博物馆1500件馆藏艺术品为样本，审计CLIP系列视觉语言模型中的社会性别偏见，发现在控制混杂因素后零样本评分呈统计等价，但该等价源于指标灵敏度不足而非模型真正公平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 审计视觉语言模型的社会偏见时，需区分算法层面的直接估值差异与档案元数据中内嵌的混杂因素。现有零样本评估往往忽略馆藏元数据的结构性偏差（如匿名与未归属作品），可能把混淆效应误读为模型偏见或公平。

Method: 构建定量审计框架，使用Met Open Access馆藏历史艺术品元数据（N=1500；有署名作品743件，男534、女209；匿名618件，占41.2%）。在三个语义提示对（杰作、质量、影响力）上测取零样本CLIP logit差分分数，比较OpenAI CLIP与OpenCLIP，采用TOST等价检验（Cohen's d ≥ 0.25）与多元OLS回归控制艺术媒介、创作年代与长宽比。

Result: 未调整评估显示评分高度收敛，无显著性别主效应（OpenAI CLIP：μ_F=-0.0067 vs μ_M=-0.0035, p=0.1829；OpenCLIP：μ_F=0.0171 vs μ_M=0.0237, p=0.1224），TOST在d≥0.25界内确认等价（pTOST<0.005）。多元OLS中艺术家性别的条件效应不显著（p>0.20），模型R²<0.02，残差嵌入方差极高，说明全局零样本估值指标接近嵌入噪声下限。

Conclusion: 宏观层面的分数等价反映的是度量指标对细粒度视觉-语义特征不敏感，并不排除局部微观层面的视觉偏见；排除41.2%未归属藏品会引入机构性幸存者偏差。评估文化遗产领域AI公平性时，必须引入多元混杂控制、等价检验与档案来源审计。

Abstract: Auditing vision-language models (VLMs) for societal bias requires distinguishing direct algorithmic valuation disparities from confounders embedded within archival metadata. In this study, we audit Contrastive Language-Image Pretraining (CLIP) models using historical artwork metadata from the Metropolitan Museum of Art Open Access collection (N = 1,500 total objects; N = 743 attributed works: Male n = 534, Female n = 209; n = 618 anonymous).
  We establish a quantitative audit framework evaluating zero-shot CLIP logit differential scores across three semantic prompt pairs (masterpiece, quality, and influence). Unadjusted evaluations demonstrate high score convergence without a statistically significant main gender effect under OpenAI CLIP (mu_F = -0.0067 vs mu_M = -0.0035, p = 0.1829) or OpenCLIP (mu_F = 0.0171 vs mu_M = 0.0237, p = 0.1224). Two One-Sided Tests (TOST) confirm statistical equivalence across Cohen's d >= 0.25 bounds (pTOST < 0.005).
  Multivariate OLS regression controlling for artwork medium, creation era, and aspect ratio (R^2 < 0.02) confirms that artist gender has no statistically significant conditional effect (p > 0.20). High residual embedding variance (R^2 < 2%) indicates that global zero-shot valuation metrics operate near an embedding noise floor, showing that broad zero-shot prompt logit differentials are a coarse measurement instrument rather than proving absolute model fairness.
  We highlight two key caveats: (i) macro-level score equivalence reflects metric insensitivity to fine-grained visual-semantic features and does not preclude localized micro-level visual biases, and (ii) excluding 41.2% unattributed holdings reflects institutional survival bias. These results demonstrate the necessity of multivariate confound control, equivalence testing, and archival provenance auditing when assessing AI fairness in cultural heritage collections.

</details>


### [107] [Temperon: Full-Time SAM Quality at a Third Less Wall-Clock](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17575)
*Stamatis Mastromichalakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Temperon：前 43% 训练预算用普通 SGD 探索，剩余全程余弦退火交给 SAM 包裹的 Muon 精修器，单次调度切换即可在多数数据上更快达到目标精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAM 使每步训练成本翻倍，但其收益集中在训练末期，因此需要研究"昂贵训练模式应花在何处"，以在同等成本下获得更高精度或更快达标。

Method: Temperon 将 epoch 预算分为两段：前 43% 用 plain SGD 探索，随后一次性切换，把整个最终余弦退火阶段交给 SAM 包裹的 Muon 精修。成本以 epoch-to-target 乘以空载 GPU 校准的 epoch 成本度量。

Result: 在 CIFAR-10/100、SVHN、Tiny ImageNet（5 个种子）上，Temperon 精度与全时 SAM 最佳配方持平，并在其中三个数据集上分别提前 35%、34%、32% 达到最难目标；比已发表的 SAM+SGD 配方高一个层级。消融显示 Muon 精修器贡献 +0.85pp，而探索器形状与重启无贡献并被撤回。与晚期 SAM 对比：对手在中等目标上最快，但 Muon 带来的层级（CIFAR-100 0.83、CIFAR-10 0.97）任何 SGD 精修法都达不到；Tiny ImageNet 上 Muon 无增益，对手胜出。该分配律可迁移到 GPT-2 预训练（全 SAM 质量下节省 29% 墙钟时间）和 GLUE 微调（SAM 成本三分之一且不劣于全时 SAM）。

Conclusion: 两个常数支配其经济性：跳过早期 SAM 获得固定额度，Muon epoch 在四个数据集上均为 SAM+SGD epoch 的 1.50 倍。由于余弦调度下精度曲线呈平台后突增，切换点无法从轨迹推断，信息存在于调度中，故按调度切换是有原理依据的。代码与可 pip 安装的实现已发布。

Abstract: Sharpness-aware minimization (SAM) doubles the cost of every training step, yet its benefit concentrates where training ends. We study where an expensive training mode should be spent and propose Temperon: a plain-SGD explorer for the first 43% of the epoch budget, then one scheduled hand-off that gives the entire final cosine anneal to a SAM-wrapped Muon refiner. On CIFAR-10/100, SVHN and Tiny ImageNet (five seeds, times reported as epochs-to-target times an idle-GPU-calibrated epoch cost), Temperon matches the best full-time-SAM recipe on accuracy everywhere while reaching the hardest common target 35%, 34% and 32% sooner on three of the four, and sits a tier above the published SAM+SGD recipe at level cost. Ablations make the attribution exact: the Muon refiner is worth +0.85pp with everything else fixed; the explorer's shape and its restarts are worth nothing, and we withdraw them as contributions. Re-running the closest rival, late-phase SAM, at matched budget shows the frontier: it is fastest to every mid-level target, but the tier the Muon refiner buys (0.83 on CIFAR-100, 0.97 on CIFAR-10) is reached by no SGD-refined method in any seed, and on Tiny ImageNet, where Muon buys no tier, the rival simply wins -- the measured boundary of the method. The allocation law transfers to GPT-2 pretraining (full-SAM quality at -29% wall-clock) and GLUE fine-tuning (never worse than full-time SAM at a third of its SAM cost). Two constants organize the economics: skipping SAM early buys a fixed credit, and a Muon epoch costs 1.50x a SAM+SGD epoch on all four datasets. Finally, the hand-off cannot be timed from the trajectory: under cosine schedules the accuracy curve is plateau-then-surge, so the information lives in the schedule, making the scheduled switch principled rather than convenient. Code and a pip-installable implementation are released.

</details>


### [108] [When the Gradient Sees Rank: Provable Necessity, Causal Recruitment, and Composition in Trained Matrix Memories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17594)
*Samuel Larson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 矩阵记忆在梯度训练下能学到随绑定数 $K$ 增长的有效秩，恢复转变发生在秩接近 $K$ 处，且学习到的算子在实体子空间近似理想循环，但在高维下恢复能力下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究使用矩阵增强推理器处理一个允许秩-1 解的任务，因此"基于梯度的训练能否学到存储和组合关联所需的秩"这一问题仍未解答。本文旨在探究矩阵记忆能否通过梯度训练学到足以精确线性恢复 $K$ 个键值绑定的秩。

Method: 在 $K$ 个新键值绑定上训练矩阵记忆，其精确线性恢复要求 $\mathrm{rank}(Z) \geq K$。使用固定线性读出查询单个矩阵状态，不接触原始绑定。以余弦相似度大于 0.9 作为恢复判据。测量不同 $K$ 下的学习有效秩，并施加训练时的秩上限以观察恢复转变，同时检验训练算子在实体子空间上的有效秩与自应用表现。

Result: 学习到的有效秩随 $K$ 增大而增加（$d=16$ 时 Spearman $ρ=1.0$）。秩上限产生恢复转变：$d=8$、$K=4$ 时，秩 3 最多恢复 0.0004，秩 4 则达 0.97。五个种子中有四个在 21 次算子自应用中保持至少 0.9996 的恢复率。实体子空间上学习算子的有效秩接近 $K$ 并近似理想循环，对单个收敛但上限低于 $K$ 的种子，基于实体子空间算子与理想循环的计算能在七次应用内将测得余弦误差预测到 0.008 以内。延长训练可解决部分初始失败，但在编码器宽度固定时，矩阵维度增大仍导致恢复能力下降。

Conclusion: 梯度训练能够学到与所需秩 $K$ 相匹配的有效秩，恢复能力在秩接近 $K$ 时发生转变，学习算子在实体子空间近似理想循环并可支持多次自应用。但恢复能力随矩阵维度增大而下降，表明该能力在高维下仍受限。

Abstract: Can gradient-based training learn the rank needed to store and compose associations in a matrix memory? In our earlier study, we used a matrix-augmented reasoner on a task that admits a rank-1 solution, leaving this question open. We train matrix memories on $K$ fresh key-value bindings whose exact linear recovery requires $\mathrm{rank}(Z) \geq K$. A fixed linear readout queries a single matrix state without access to the original bindings. Experiments measure recovery by cosine similarity greater than 0.9, a threshold distinct from mathematical equality. Learned effective rank increases with $K$ across the tested grid (Spearman $ρ= 1.0$ at $d = 16$). Training-time rank caps produce a recovery transition near $k = K$: at $d = 8$, $K = 4$, rank 3 gives at most 0.0004 recovery and rank 4 gives 0.97. Four of five seeds retain at least 0.9996 recovery through 21-fold self-application of the trained operator. On the entity subspace, the learned operator has effective rank close to $K$ and approximates the ideal cycle. For the single converged seed capped below $K$, a calculation using the entity-subspace operator and ideal cycle predicts the measured cosine within 0.008 through seven applications. Extending training resolves several initial failures, but recovery still declines at larger matrix dimensions with encoder width fixed.

</details>


### [109] [Prior-Free Competitive Ratios for Improving Bandits: Scale, Curvature and Horizon Are Free, but Not Jointly Under Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17595)
*Xuan Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究改进型多臂老虎机问题，提出probe-and-commit算法，消除对数因子，并确定各时间范围的最优竞争比Θ(√k+k/T)，涵盖噪声与无噪声情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Blum和Ravichandran（ALT 2025）证明随机算法在已知尺度m时达到O(√k)近似，未知尺度时为O(√k log k)，但该对数因子并非必要；同时问题在无噪声、未知先验及噪声情形下的最优比率尚未明确。

Method: 提出一页的probe-and-commit算法，无需知道尺度m即可达到4√3√k竞争比；进一步提出随机边缘探测算法（无需尺度m、凹包指数β或时间范围T）以及嵌套随机排列探测算法，并分析均匀适应代价φ_ε(k)。

Result: probe-and-commit在T≥2⌊√k⌋时达到4√3√k竞争比，并确定所有时间范围的最优比率为Θ(√k+k/T)；无噪声时随机边缘探测算法同时对所有β和时间范围达到Θ(k^{β/(1+β)}+k/T)；噪声模型下probe-and-commit保持Θ(√k+k/T)，均匀适应代价φ_ε(k)=Θ_ε(√(log k/log log k))，已知m或β之一则恢复常数代价。

Conclusion: 对数因子可被消除，probe-and-commit算法无需先验知识即可达到最优或接近最优竞争比；在噪声情形下，同时未知m和β会带来额外的适应代价，但仅知其一即可保持常数代价。

Abstract: In the improving multi-armed bandits problem, each of $k$ arms has an unknown nondecreasing, discretely concave reward curve $f_i$, and pulling arm $i$ for the $t$-th time yields $f_i(t)$. For sufficiently long horizons, Blum and Ravichandran (ALT 2025) proved that randomized algorithms achieve an $O(\sqrt k)$ approximation to the best single arm when the scale $m=f^*(T)$ of the optimal arm is known ($T\ge2k$), and $O(\sqrt k\log k)$ when it is not ($T>4k$), against an $Ω(\sqrt k)$ lower bound.
