Result: 理论上证明了根节点值估计的收敛率为O(n^{-1/2})，与标准MCTS相当；实验证明在奖励分布和转移动态存在显著歧义时仍能实现鲁棒的规划性能。

Conclusion: 该工作为仿真到现实部署提供了一种具备理论收敛保证的鲁棒MCTS方法，有效提升了不确定环境下的规划鲁棒性。

Abstract: Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) is a powerful framework for solving complex decision-making problems, yet it often relies on the assumption that the simulator and the real-world dynamics are identical. Although this assumption helps achieve the success of MCTS in games like Chess, Go, and Shogi, the real-world scenarios incur ambiguity due to their modeling mismatches in low-fidelity simulators. In this work, we present a new robust variant of MCTS that mitigates dynamical model ambiguities. Our algorithm addresses transition dynamics and reward distribution ambiguities to bridge the gap between simulation-based planning and real-world deployment. We incorporate a robust power mean backup operator and carefully designed exploration bonuses to ensure finite-sample convergence at every node in the search tree. We show that our algorithm achieves a convergence rate of $\mathcal{O}(n^{-1/2})$ for the value estimation at the root node, comparable to that of standard MCTS. Finally, we provide empirical evidence that our method achieves robust performance in planning problems even under significant ambiguity in the underlying reward distribution and transition dynamics.

</details>


### [179] [A Geometric Theory of Decision Boundaries in Structured Markov Decision Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18610)
*Fredy Pokou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出结构化最优策略的几何理论，以策略诱导的决策边界几何为核心分析对象，证明其是策略重构所需的最小表示，并决定重构问题的统计与计算复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典动态规划通过值函数和策略来表示最优序贯决策，但这种函数式表示虽然利于计算最优决策，却无法直接揭示在固定最优策略后，支配策略重构、表示复杂度或预言机查询复杂度的数学对象。

Method: 在适当的结构正则条件下，引入策略诱导的决策边界几何作为主要分析对象；建立策略诱导决策几何的结构性质，提出内蕴的边界与决策复杂度概念，推导决策压缩的信息论度量，并获得从黑箱策略查询进行边界估计与策略重构的统计保证。

Result: 所考虑的结构化决策问题中，策略重构复杂度由决策边界几何支配，而非由环境状态空间的基数决定；控制性数值实验检验了主要理论预测并提供了与框架一致的实证证据。

Conclusion: 该几何理论表明，决策边界几何是策略重构的最小表示，并决定其统计与计算复杂度，为理解最优策略的表示与重构提供了新的几何视角。

Abstract: Classical dynamic programming represents optimal sequential decisions through value functions and policies. While this functional representation is natural for computing optimal decisions, it does not directly identify the mathematical object governing policy reconstruction, representation complexity, or oracle-query complexity once an optimal policy is fixed. This paper addresses this question by developing a geometric theory of structured optimal policies in which the decision-boundary geometry induced by the policy becomes the primary object of analysis. We show that, under suitable structural regularity conditions, this geometry provides the minimal representation required for policy reconstruction and determines the statistical and computational complexity of the reconstruction problem. Building upon this representation, we establish structural properties of policy-induced decision geometry, introduce intrinsic notions of boundary and decision complexity, derive information-theoretic measures of decision compression, and obtain statistical guarantees for boundary estimation and policy reconstruction from black-box policy queries. Collectively, these results demonstrate that, for the structured decision problems considered here, the complexity of policy reconstruction is governed by the geometry of the decision boundary rather than by the cardinality of the ambient state space. Controlled numerical experiments examine the principal theoretical predictions and provide empirical evidence consistent with the proposed framework.

</details>


### [180] [Weakening Neurons: An Input-Output Functionality in Transformers with Outsize Influence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18612)
*Sebastian Gerstner,Hilal AlQuabeh,Kentaro Inui,Hinrich Schütze*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过计算GLU神经元读写权重向量的余弦相似度，发现LLM中普遍存在"弱化神经元"，且它们位于后层、激活频繁、对模型行为影响大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分析大语言模型中GLU神经元的输入-输出行为，理解神经元如何读写残差流中的信息，尤其是此前被忽视的负余弦相似度神经元的功能。

Method: 提出简单分析方法：对每个神经元计算其输入（读取）与输出（写入）权重向量之间的余弦相似度。强负相似度表示神经元削弱其在残差流中检测到的方向，称为弱化神经元；对应地，正相似度为（条件性）强化神经元。

Result: 九个不同的LLM呈现相似模式：弱化神经元主要出现在后层，而强化神经元多见于早中期层；弱化神经元数量虽少，但激活频繁且对模型行为影响大；当门控值为负时，弱化神经元对模型输出有强影响。

Conclusion: 弱化神经元是LLM中一类被忽视但功能重要的神经元，其负门控值也能编码功能，表明神经元读写行为分析能为理解LLM内部机制提供新视角。

Abstract: We analyze the learned input-output behavior of GLU-based neurons in large language models (LLMs). We propose a simple analysis method: For each neuron, we compute the cosine similarities between its input (reading) and output (writing) weight vectors. In this scheme, a strong negative cosine similarity indicates the neuron weakens the direction it detects in the residual stream, so we call this a weakening neuron. This allows us to gain a number of novel insights. First, we show that nine different LLMs have similar patterns: weakening neurons appear mostly in late layers whereas their counterparts, (conditional) strengthening neurons, are frequent in early-middle layers. Second, we find that weakening neurons display surprising behavior: even though there are few, they activate often and have a large influence on model behavior. Third, weakening neurons have a strong effect on model output when gate values are negative -- which is surprising since negative gate values are not expected to encode functionality.

</details>


### [181] [How Many Labels Does Model Choice Need? Certificates and Budgets for Selective Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18622)
*Tetsuji Kuboyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文量化了比较分类器选择性性能所需的标签预算，为AUGRC建立了预标签下界、证书大小上界与精确停止检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分类器可产生相同预测，但要比较其选择性性能仍需标签：置信度排序会以不同方式加权同样的错误。论文旨在量化这一标签需求，并建立带证书的模型比较方法。

Method: 针对广义风险-覆盖率曲线下面积（AUGRC），提出预标签下界以排除预算不足；在标签全知时，用覆盖线性规划将固定胜者所需最少标签数（证书大小）在K个候选中界定在K-1以内；并分析独立均匀顺序、相同预测及iid Bernoulli错误下的精确获取策略，提出适用于任意获取顺序的精确停止检验。

Result: 固定K时预标签下界趋近池的四分之一；iid Bernoulli错误下精确策略渐近需读取几乎所有标签，而两候选证书只需一半。九个数据集108个特征面板比较中，分歧标签可决定所有精度选择但无法决定任何AUGRC选择；96种条件下20%预算被排除，证书平均需56-57%。十个预训练图像分类器条件下，精确选择需读取10,000个标签的68-91%，AUGRC容忍5e-4时降至50-67%。

Conclusion: 研究结果将置信度排序与标签预算及可认证的模型比较联系起来，表明AUGRC选择比精度选择需要更多标签，且证书与停止检验为有限预算下的可信比较提供了工具。

Abstract: Classifiers can make identical predictions yet require labels to compare their selective performance: confidence ranks weight the same errors differently. We quantify this requirement for the area under the generalized risk-coverage curve (AUGRC). A prelabel lower bound rules out insufficient budgets. With all labels known, a covering linear program bounds the minimum number of labels sufficient to fix the winner (the certificate size) within $K-1$ labels for $K$ candidates. For fixed $K$, independent uniform orders and identical predictions, the prelabel bound approaches one quarter of the pool. With iid Bernoulli errors independent of the orders, every exact acquisition policy reads almost all labels asymptotically, although a two-candidate certificate needs only half. Across 108 feature-panel comparisons on nine datasets, disagreement labels settle every accuracy choice but no AUGRC choice. A 20% budget is ruled out in 96 conditions; certificates need 56-57% on average. On ten conditions with pretrained image classifiers, confidence-score choice reads 68-91% of 10,000 labels for exact selection and 50-67% with AUGRC tolerance $5\times10^{-4}$. An exact stopping test works with any acquisition order. Together, these results link confidence ranks to label budgets and certified model comparison.

</details>


### [182] [CoRe-MARL: Cooperative Redistribution Under Unknown Dynamics Using Recurrent Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18639)
*Naimur Rahman Chowdhury,Shatabdi Sen Prapti,Md. Salehin Seyam,Limon Bin Hossain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CoRe-MARL协作式多智能体强化学习框架，将应急物资再分配建模为Dec-POMDP，用循环网络+MAPPO在不确定动态下实现去中心化执行，缩小区域间服务差距并改善最差区域服务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 应急管理援助项目（如物资发放）对受灾社区至关重要，但本地中心处于去中心化网络，面临不确定的需求与供给动态，导致本地服务可用性不一致。中心间物资再分配可缓解失衡，但各中心独立决策、信息有限、交通中断，因此需要一种能在不确定和动态演化环境下协作再分配的方法。

Method: 提出CoRe-MARL协作式多智能体强化学习框架：(1) 将问题形式化为去中心化部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（Dec-POMDP），每个中心作为一个智能体学习再分配策略；(2) 目标为改善最差区域服务、缩小区域间服务差距，同时保护全网服务；(3) 引入循环神经网络（recurrent network）在不直接观测的情况下捕捉演化的供需动态；(4) 采用多智能体近端策略优化（MAPPO），实现集中训练、去中心化执行（CTDE）。

Result: 在具有多样轨迹的仿真环境中评估，其中精确动态不被执行者（actors）和MAPPO评论家（critic）观测。与循环独立PPO（recurrent IPPO）和纯本地启发式方法相比，循环MAPPO降低了各本地中心间的服务差距，提升了最差服务中心的水平，同时保持有竞争力的全网服务。循环MAPPO在多种轨迹模式下表现一致，表明其适应演化动态的能力。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，协作学习可用于去中心化再分配，在不确定且不断演化的动态环境下提升服务的公平性。

Abstract: Emergency management assistance programs, such as relief distribution, are essential for delivering necessary supplies to affected communities. However, these programs operate in a decentralized network of local centers that face uncertain local demand and supply dynamics, resulting in inconsistent avail- ability of local services. Redistribution of supplies among these local centers reduces these imbalances, but the centers often make decisions independently, with limited information and disrupted transportation. This study develops CoRe-MARL, a cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) framework, by formulating a decentralized partially observable Markov decision process (Dec-POMDP). We treat each center as an agent that learns a redistribution policy to improve the service in the worst-case region and reduce the service gap across regions while protecting network-wide service. We incorporate a recurrent network that captures evolving supply and demand dynamics without direct observation, while multi-agent proximal policy optimization (MAPPO) enables centralized training and decentralized execution (CTDE). We evaluate the framework in a simulated environment with diverse trajectories, where exact dynamics are not observed by actors and the MAPPO critic. We compare the recurrent MAPPO with the recurrent independent PPO (IPPO) and a local only heuristic, and find that MAPPO reduces the service gap across local centers and enhances service for the worst-served center while maintaining competitive network-wide service. The recurrent MAPPO also shows consistent performance across diverse trajectory patterns, demonstrating its ability to adapt to evolving dynamics. The findings demonstrate the capability of cooperative learning for decentralized redistribution and improving equitable service under uncertain and evolving dynamics.

</details>


### [183] [Learning to Program Adaptive Non-Local Observables for Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18655)
*Yu-Ting Lee,Samuel Yen-Chi Chen,Huan-Hsin Tseng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出QFWP-ANO架构，用经典超网络根据输入动态生成变分量子电路参数与自适应非局域可观测量，在时间序列预测和强化学习任务中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子神经网络通常基于变分量子电路，受限于局部测量；自适应非局域可观测量（ANO）虽通过联合优化电路参数与多量子比特测量缓解该问题，但现有ANO方法仅学习单一静态可观测量，对所有输入保持不变，缺乏输入适应性。

Method: 提出QFWP-ANO架构：引入经典超网络，以每个输入为条件，动态编程变分量子电路的参数和/或非局域可观测量，实现输入条件化的自适应非局域测量。

Result: 在四个ETT数据集的多元时间序列预测中，QFWP-ANO在20个设定中有16个取得最低MSE，其余4个为次低，优于ANO及其他强基线；在强化学习任务上持续超越ANO-VQC。

Conclusion: 结果表明，输入条件化的自适应非局域可观测量是增强量子神经网络的有效途径。

Abstract: Quantum neural networks (QNNs) are typically built from variational quantum circuits (VQCs), which are limited by local measurements. Adaptive non-local observables (ANO) address this by jointly optimizing circuit parameters and multi-qubit measurements. However, existing ANO-based VQCs learn only a single static observable that remains invariant across all inputs. We propose QFWP-ANO, a novel architecture which employs a classical hypernetwork to dynamically program VQC parameters and/or non-local observables conditioned on each input. On multivariate time-series forecasting across four ETT datasets, QFWP-ANO achieves the lowest MSE in 16 of 20 settings and second-lowest in the remaining four, surpassing ANO-based and other strong baselines. On reinforcement learning tasks, QFWP-ANO consistently surpasses ANO-VQCs. Our results establish input-conditioned ANO as an effective approach for enhancing QNNs.

</details>


### [184] [Revisiting Distributed Sign-Based Variance Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18656)
*Wei Jiang,Zechao Li,Lijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文解决了基于符号（sign）的分布式优化在数据异构时的偏差问题，提出了在服务器端通过无偏压缩递归梯度增量来跟踪全局梯度的方法，获得了非凸随机和有限和优化下的最优收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于符号的方法能降低分布式环境中的通信成本，但当数据异构时，聚合本地符号会引入偏差，导致现有的基于符号的方差缩减方法无法获得最优收敛率。作者旨在解决这一偏差问题并达到最优收敛率。

Method: 首先给出一个反例，说明即使使用精确的本地梯度，多数投票也可能无法逼近稳定点。受此局限启发，提出在服务器端通过无偏压缩递归梯度增量来跟踪全局梯度。对于有限和问题，结合周期性精确梯度刷新与压缩的组件梯度差异。

Result: 获得了非凸随机优化的收敛率：ℓ1范数下为O(√(d/K)+√d(a/(nK))^{1/3})，ℓ2范数下为O(√(a/K)+√a/(nK)^{1/3})，其中K为迭代次数，n为工作节点数，d为维度，a=1+ω（ω为压缩器相对方差）。对于含M个组件的有限和问题，样本复杂度分别为O(M+d√(aM)ε^{-2})和O(M+a√M ε^{-2})（对应ℓ1和ℓ2梯度范数至多ε），与集中式设置下的相应界相匹配。

Conclusion: 通过跟踪服务器端全局梯度并结合无偏压缩，本文克服了符号聚合的偏差问题，在非凸随机与有限和优化中均达到最优收敛率，且有限和问题的样本复杂度与集中式设置相匹配。

Abstract: Sign-based methods reduce communication costs in distributed environments, but aggregating local signs can introduce bias when data are heterogeneous. As a result, existing sign-based variance reduction methods fail to obtain the optimal convergence rates. In this paper, we solve this problem and obtain optimal rates for both nonconvex stochastic and finite-sum optimization. We first give a counterexample showing that majority voting can fail to approach stationary points even with exact local gradients. Motivated by this limitation, we propose tracking the global gradient at the server through unbiased compression of recursive gradient increments. As a result, we can obtain the convergence rates of $O(\sqrt{d/K}+\sqrt d (a/(nK))^{1/3})$ for the $\ell_1$-norm and $O(\sqrt{a/K}+\sqrt a/(nK)^{1/3})$ for the $\ell_2$-norm. Here, $K$ is the iteration number, $n$ is the number of workers, $d$ is the dimension, and $a=1+ω$, with $ω$ denoting the compressor's relative variance. For finite-sum problems with $M$ components, we combine periodic exact gradient refreshes with compressed component-gradient differences. The resulting total sample complexities are $O(M+d\sqrt{aM}ε^{-2})$ and $O(M+a\sqrt M\ epsilon^{-2})$ for $\ell_1$ and $\ell_2$ gradient norms at most $ε$, matching the corresponding bounds in centralized settings.

</details>


### [185] [Rethinking Critic Learning in PPO: Understanding and Mitigating Value Flattening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18708)
*Yizhuo Li,Jianhao Yan,Yun Luo,Zhi Wang,Futing Wang,Rong-Xi Tan,Kanghui Tian,Ganqu Cui,Ning Ding,Peilin Zhao,Yafu Li,Yu Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示PPO评论家中的系统性失效模式"价值扁平化"，并提出稀疏监督策略SP³O加以缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在强化学习训练大语言模型时，PPO通常使用critic估计状态价值以降低策略更新方差，但critic学习本身可能存在被忽视的失效模式，作者希望揭示并解决这一问题。

Method: 作者通过多条蒙特卡洛续写估计状态价值，发现价值突变而critic预测却相对平坦，将此现象命名为"价值扁平化"；并在可控的FrozenLake环境中验证，发现其随状态空间增大而加剧。理论分析将其归因于critic损失中的隐式方差惩罚，以及时间相关且梯度相似状态带来的冗余更新。据此提出SParse Proximal Policy Optimization（SP³O），仅在每条响应的少数间隔状态上施加价值损失。

Result: 在Qwen3-Base上的实验表明，每条响应仅监督三个状态的SP³O即可缓解价值扁平化，并在不同模型规模和评测集上持续提升学习到的策略。

Conclusion: 价值扁平化是标准PPO中critic学习的重要但被忽视的失效模式，简单的稀疏监督策略可有效缓解该问题并提升策略表现。

Abstract: In reinforcement learning for large language models, Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) commonly uses a critic to estimate state values and reduce the variance of policy updates. However, we uncover a systematic failure mode in PPO critics, which we call Value Flattening: state values, estimated from multiple Monte Carlo continuations, change sharply across intermediate states while critic predictions remain comparatively flat. We further observe this phenomenon in a controlled FrozenLake environment and find that it becomes more pronounced as the state space grows. Our theoretical and empirical analyses relate Value Flattening to an implicit variance penalty in the critic loss and redundant updates from temporally correlated states with similar gradients. Motivated by these findings, we introduce SParse Proximal Policy Optimization (SP$^3$O), which applies the value loss to only a few well-separated states in each response to mitigate both effects. Experiments on Qwen3-Base show that SP$^3$O with only three states supervised per response can mitigate Value Flattening and consistently improve the learned policy across model sizes and evaluation suites. Together, our results identify Value Flattening as an important yet overlooked failure mode of critic learning in standard PPO and show that a simple sparse supervision strategy can mitigate it.

</details>


### [186] [When Edit Flows are Edit Jumps: replicating Edit Flows and EvoFlows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18745)
*Gabriel Bénédict,Melanie Buechler,Gerard Riera-Solà,Chloé de Ancos,Yves Gaetan Nana Teukam,Moritz Freidank*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 EditJumps，统一了 Edit Flows 与 EvoFlows 的连续时间跳跃过程框架，并首次开源实现，用单一天然抗体编辑器实现零样本编辑未见先导序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抗体先导优化需要在已有候选序列上做有界的小规模编辑（替换、插入、删除）。基于编辑的生成模型是唯一能在不预先固定编辑位置、编辑数量和输出长度的情况下分配编辑预算的方法。然而现有方法 Edit Flows 和 EvoFlows 未公开代码或完整训练规范，导致难以复现，并且需要按家族重新训练。

Method: 作者证明 Edit Flows 与 EvoFlows 遵循同一底层过程：编辑以学习到的速率在连续时间中逐个触发，即有限序列上 generator matching 的纯跳跃情形。据此提出 EditJumps，首个该框架的开源实现，使用在 166 万 Observed Antibody Space 同源对上训练的单一天然抗体编辑器，为种子序列生成同源样变体。

Result: 该通用编辑器可零样本编辑未见先导序列，无需原方法所需的按家族重训练。复现过程揭示：为对齐已发表的编辑分布，需逆向工程出一个未公开的速率缩放超参数，它决定实际突变数量；此外，已发表的评价指标对参考样本量高度敏感，方法排序经常发生翻转。

Conclusion: EditJumps 提供了统一框架的开源实现，证明开源代码对生成生物学至关重要，并公开完整代码库、自动化测试套件和配置（https://github.com/VisiumCH/editjumps）。

Abstract: Antibody lead optimization calls for a small, bounded set of edits to an existing candidate: substitutions, but also insertions and deletions. Edit-based generative models are the only ones that allocate such an edit budget without fixing the edit positions, the edit count, or the output length in advance. However, the existing approaches Edit Flows and EvoFlows did not release code or complete training specifications. Here, we show that both methods follow the same underlying process -- edits firing one at a time, at learned rates, in continuous time -- the pure-jump case of generator matching over finite sequences. With EditJumps we introduce the first open implementation of this framework, with a single generalist antibody editor trained on 1.66M Observed Antibody Space homolog pairs to propose homolog-like variants of a seed sequence, editing unseen leads zero-shot, without the per-family retraining original approaches require. Replicating this system from scratch exposes why open code is essential for generative biology: reconciling published edit distributions required reverse-engineering an undocumented rate-scaling hyperparameter that dictates realized mutation counts. Moreover, we show that published evaluation metrics are highly sensitive to reference sample size, frequently flipping method rankings. We release our full codebase, automated test suite, and configurations at: https://github.com/VisiumCH/editjumps

</details>


### [187] [A Convergence Framework for Deep $V$-Learning: Error Propagation and Sharp Action-Gap Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18782)
*Yury Kolomeytsev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为 horizon 为 H 的深度 V-learning 建立了收敛界，将更新误差分解为六类残差，并在集中性、边际条件、覆盖与网络逼近等假设下给出策略损失与统计误差的显式界，包括跨 horizon 采样分配与残余衰减判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度 V-learning 在实践中广泛应用，但缺乏对固定 horizon 下收敛性、误差来源分解与统计速率的系统理论刻画，尤其是跨 horizon 采样分配与残余误差衰减条件的量化。

Method: 算法用预测模型与值函数选动作，并拟合标量值函数到已执行转移目标。将更新误差分解为拟合、转移复用、目标构造、回放、动作选择、探索六类残差；在 L^s concentrability 下用 L^p (p=s/(s-1)) 范数控制期望 L^1 策略损失，并分析跨 horizon 共享采样分布的代价、连续与整数最优分配、边际条件、冻结分数距离、生存概率与覆盖条件，以及空间 ReLU 网络与有限状态的回归速率。

Result: 界显式加权仅最后 H−1 个更新块的残差并含初始化项；统计误差 n^{-ν} 下给出最优连续分配与整数分配（目标在约束最优的 2^ν 因子内）；边际条件指数 α 下动作误差为 Λ^{1+α/p} 且一步构造证明指数尖锐；冻结与最优分数距离将最优间隙条件转移到冻结迭代；部署生存概率与覆盖条件给出近似分数策略的界；空间 ReLU 网络给出条件神经回归速率，有限状态给出无对数期望拟合速率。

Conclusion: 结果为固定 horizon、生成式重置的近似 ERM 过程在精确动作分数下提供期望策略损失一致性，并给出 FIFO/交错 SGD 的显式残余衰减判据。

Abstract: We establish convergence bounds for deep $V$-learning with horizon $H$. The algorithm fits a scalar value function to targets from executed transitions and selects actions using a predictive model and the value function. For current observed-successor targets with fresh true-kernel outcomes, the conditional mean is $\mathcal{T}^βV$, which averages over behavior-policy actions. The Bellman optimality update is $\mathcal{T} V$. We decompose the update error into six residuals: fitting, transition reuse, target construction, replay, action selection, and exploration. Under $L^s$ concentrability, their $L^p$ norms ($p=s/(s-1)$) control expected $L^1$ policy loss. The bound explicitly weights residuals from only the last $H-1$ update blocks, plus an initialization term for shorter runs. We quantify the cost of a shared sampling distribution across horizon levels. For statistical error bounds of order $n^{-ν}$, we derive optimal continuous allocations and an integer allocation whose objective is within a factor $2^ν$ of the constrained optimum. A margin condition with exponent $α$ gives action error of order $Λ^{1+α/p}$, where $Λ$ combines network drift and score error; a one-step construction proves the exponent sharp. Bounds on the distance between frozen and optimal scores transfer an optimal-gap condition to frozen-iterate gap bounds while retaining the mass of optimal ties. Survival probabilities and coverage conditions at deployment yield bounds for policies selected with approximate scores. Separate spatial ReLU networks per horizon level give a conditional neural regression rate, and the finite-state case gives a log-free expected fit rate. These results give expected policy-loss consistency for the fixed-horizon generative-reset approximate-ERM procedure with exact action scores and provide an explicit residual-decay criterion for FIFO/interleaved SGD.

</details>


### [188] [WaveTLM: Reliable Time-Series Language Modeling through Task Compilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18812)
*Jiahui Chen,Bingke Zhu,Hongyu Pan,Yingying Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出可靠时间序列语言建模概念，构建ExecTS-QA基准并提出WaveTLM编译-执行模型，契约有效覆盖率达99.40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列语言模型虽有统一自然语言接口，但生成的合理文本无法保证任务输出可靠：数值序列可能违反形状、尺度、通道顺序或时间对齐，文本决策可能超出合法标签空间，需将任务对象可靠性与预测质量分离。

Method: 提出任务编译方案WaveTLM统一编译-执行模型：任务编译器将用户请求、可见参数与波形证据转换为带类型任务状态，任务原生执行器构建数值张量、合法决策或结构化记录；并构建契约化基准ExecTS-QA，覆盖预测、插补、分类、异常检测与波形分析五类任务。

Result: 单一WaveTLM检查点在ExecTS-QA上达到99.40%契约有效覆盖率，最强字符串优先基线仅37.83%，同时在五类任务族上保持均衡预测性能；在SciTS、TSQA、IRTS-ToolBench与ARFBench上的评估提供额外迁移证据。

Conclusion: 任务编译可将看似合理的语言生成转化为可靠的时间序列输出。代码、构建脚本与ExecTS-QA数据集将在发表后公开。

Abstract: Time-series language models provide a shared natural-language interface across temporal tasks, but plausible text does not guarantee reliable task outputs. Responses may appear reasonable while hallucinating the required object: numerical sequences can violate shape, scale, channel order, or temporal alignment, and textual decisions can fall outside the legal label space. We formulate reliable time-series language modeling, separating task-object reliability from predictive quality. We introduce ExecTS-QA, a contract-grounded benchmark spanning forecasting, imputation, classification, anomaly detection, and waveform analysis. We further propose WaveTLM, a unified compiler-executor model whose task compiler transforms user requests, visible arguments, and wave-grounded evidence into typed task states, while task-native executors construct numerical tensors, legal decisions, or structured records. On ExecTS-QA, a single WaveTLM checkpoint achieves 99.40% contract-valid coverage, compared with 37.83% for the strongest evaluated string-first baseline, while retaining balanced predictive performance across all five task families. Evaluations on SciTS, TSQA, IRTS-ToolBench, and ARFBench provide additional evidence of transfer. The code, construction scripts, and ExecTS-QA dataset will be publicly released upon publication. These results show that task compilation can convert plausible language generation into reliable time-series outputs.

</details>


### [189] [Interpretable Multi-Instance Learning Enables Early Prediction of Key Molecular Alterations from Routine Flow Cytometry in Acute Myeloid Leukemia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18825)
*Jonathan Legrand,Aguirre Mimoun,Baudouin Denis de Senneville,Audrey Bidet,Pierre-Yves Dumas,Christèle Etchegaray*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 基于决策树的可解释多示例学习模型利用常规流式细胞术数据，可在数小时内预测AML患者的NPM1和FLT3-ITD突变状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NPM1和FLT3-ITD的分子检测对急性髓系白血病（AML）的早期治疗决策至关重要，但检测结果往往需要数周才能获得；而流式细胞术在入院数小时内即可完成且属常规检查，若能从中直接预测突变状态，则无需额外成本或延迟。

Method: 开发了一种基于决策树的可解释多示例学习（MIL）分类器，将每个患者样本建模为单个细胞的集合，并由细胞级预测推断突变状态。模型与基于临床变量的随机森林以及适配多管流式细胞术数据的深度卷积神经网络进行基准比较。在197例发现队列上做交叉验证，并在161例独立队列上测试，采用AUROC和阳性预测值评估性能。

Result: 发现队列交叉验证中，MIL模型对NPM1的平均AUROC为0.96（SD=0.05），对FLT3-ITD为0.86（SD=0.10），优于临床基线并与深度学习方法相当。在161例独立测试队列中泛化良好，AUROC分别为0.90（NPM1）和0.82（FLT3-ITD），阳性预测值分别为0.87和0.68。细胞级解释还原了已知的免疫表型特征（NPM1突变为CD33+/CD34-，FLT3-ITD为CD33+/低侧向散射），将模型预测与已知生物学直接关联。

Conclusion: 可解释模型应用于常规诊疗中已采集的数据，可在数小时内预测AML分子状态，为更早、基于生物学的治疗决策提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Background: Molecular testing for NPM1 and FLT3-ITD mutations guides critical early treatment decisions in acute myeloid leukemia (AML), but results can take weeks, long after these decisions must be made. Flow cytometry, already performed within hours of admission as part of routine care, may carry enough signal to predict these mutations directly, without added cost or delay. Methods: We developed an interpretable multi-instance learning classifier based on a decision tree, in which each patient sample is modeled as a collection of individual cells and mutation status is inferred from cell-level predictions. The model was benchmarked against a random forest trained on clinical variables and a deep convolutional neural network adapted for multitube flow cytometry data. Performance was assessed by cross-validation on a discovery cohort of 197 patients and tested on an independent cohort of 161 patients, using the area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUROC) and positive predictive value. Results: In cross-validation on the discovery cohort, the MIL model achieved mean AUROCs of 0.96 (SD=0.05) for NPM1 and 0.86 (SD=0.10) for FLT3-ITD, outperforming the clinical baseline and matching deep learning approaches. The model then successfully generalized to the independent test cohort of 161 patients, reaching AUROCs of 0.90 (NPM1) and 0.82 (FLT3-ITD), with positive predictive values of 0.87 and 0.68, respectively. Cell-level interpretation recovered established immunophenotypic signatures (CD33${}^{+}$ /CD34___ for NPM1-mutated cases, CD33${}^{+}$ /low side-scatter for FLT3-ITD), directly linking model predictions to known biology.  Conclusions: These results show that an interpretable model applied to data already collected in routine care can predict AML molecular status within hours, offering a practical route to earlier, biology-informed treatment decisions.

</details>


### [190] [Physics-based prediction, uncertainty quantification and decision-making for IN718 crystallographic texture intensity across LPBF defocus regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18863)
*Yisheng Lu,John Riris,Jie Song,Yao Fu,Jie Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种两阶段物理驱动模型，用于预测激光粉末床熔融Inconel 718中<001>||BD织构，通过结合经验物理模型与随机森林残差修正，并引入数据适用性、物理有效性与预测不确定性的分离判定机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑箱模型在分布偏移下可能失效，且无法区分"数据支持不足"与"物理有效性丧失"，因此需要一种可迁移、可拒判的可靠织构预测方法，以关联工艺条件与各向异性响应并支持工艺合格性认证。

Method: 构建两阶段模型：第一阶段将工艺变量映射到熔化模式与熔池几何；第二阶段结合经验物理模型与随机森林残差模型预测织构。采用k近邻权重衰减低支持度查询的残差修正，并以面积束功率密度准则在传导包络之外拒绝预测。在物理有效集上评估共形区间，并用SHAP与Sobol分析考察残差敏感性。

Result: 在受控的留一散焦评估中，物理锚定模型R^2=0.778，黑箱模型为-0.001，门控混合模型为0.750；留一组交叉验证下门控混合模型R^2=0.592，黑箱为0.538。分组交叉验证保留集覆盖率为92.9%（平均全宽3.65 MUD），迁移至+80 mm散焦区间时覆盖率达100%（宽度3.21 MUD）。示例映射得到的保留BD弹性模量跨度为127–187 GPa。在独立样本集的九个条件中，框架拒判3个、衰减3个，其余与实测排序一致。

Conclusion: 将数据适用性、物理有效性与预测不确定性拆分为独立决策，使框架能在无约束模型失效的场景下实现迁移，并在无模型类别表现充分时主动拒判预测。

Abstract: Reliable prediction of crystallographic texture in laser powder bed fusion is critical for linking process conditions with anisotropic response and for qualification. However, black-box models may fail under shift and cannot distinguish weak data support from loss of physical validity. This study develops a two-stage physics-based model for <001> || BD (build direction) texture in Inconel 718. Stage 1 maps process variables to melting mode and melt pool geometry. Stage 2 predicts texture by combining an empirical physics model with a random-forest residual model. A k-nearest-neighbor weight attenuates residual corrections for poorly supported queries, while a study-specific areal beam-power-density criterion withholds predictions outside the adopted conduction envelope. Conformal intervals are evaluated on the retained physics-valid set, and SHAP and Sobol analyses assess residual sensitivity. Under a controlled leave-one-defocus-out evaluation, the physics anchor achieved R^2 = 0.778, against -0.001 for the black-box model and 0.750 for the gated hybrid. Under leave-one-group-out cross-validation, the gated hybrid reached R^2 = 0.592 against 0.538 for the black-box model. Retained-set coverage was 92.9% at a mean full width of 3.65 multiples of a uniform distribution (MUD) under grouped cross-validation and 100% at a width of 3.21 MUD under transfer to a withheld +80 mm defocus regime. An illustrative mapping produced a retained BD elastic-modulus span of 127-187 GPa. On nine conditions from a separately built sample set, the framework withheld three, attenuated three, and matched the measured ordering for the rest. Separating data applicability, physics validity, and predictive uncertainty into distinct decisions lets the framework transfer where an unconstrained model does not, and withhold predictions where no model class performs adequately.

</details>


### [191] [Preventing Model Collapse: A Fisher-Rao Perspective on the Dynamics of Training with Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18878)
*Matteo Marchi,João Pedro Silvestre,Bahman Gharesifard,Paulo Tabuada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究合成数据与人类数据混合训练中防止模型崩溃所需的人类数据最小比例，利用概率单纯形上的Fisher-Rao信息几何结构给出更紧的理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型训练已耗尽高质量人类数据，转而大量使用合成数据；但递归训练合成数据会引发模型崩溃，即模型逐渐遗忘真实数据分布。虽然混合新鲜人类数据可以缓解该问题，但维持训练稳定所需的人类与合成数据最小比例尚无确切理论答案，且已有下界在高维情形下会变得空洞无用。

Method: 作者放弃传统的R^n欧氏度量分析，转而显式利用类别概率分布空间即概率单纯形的信息几何结构，在Fisher-Rao度量下分析训练过程的动力学，推导出定量化的收缩界与不变性界。

Result: 所推导的收缩与不变性界在维度增加时保持稳定且不会退化为平凡结果，从而说明防止模型崩溃的有效所需数据比例与既有工作所隐含的结论不同。

Conclusion: 通过Fisher-Rao度量下的信息几何分析，本文为合成与人类数据混合训练中防止模型崩溃提供了更严谨且对高维情形有效的理论保证，纠正了以往基于欧氏度量得到的数据比例下界。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are now routinely trained using synthetic data, since high-quality human data has been exhausted by the ever increasing needs of larger and larger models. However, recursive training on synthetic data frequently induces model collapse, a degenerative feedback loop where models progressively forget the true underlying data distribution. Training on a mixture of synthetic and fresh human data is a logical countermeasure and can prevent model collapse. However, it is an open question as to what is the exact minimum required ratio of human-to-synthetic data to maintain training stability. In this paper, we establish rigorous theoretical guarantees on the minimum rate of human data required to prevent model collapse. Although previous work established a formal lower bound for this ratio, such bound can be vacuous for very high dimensions, as the analysis relies on the usual Euclidean metric in R^n and is not adapted to the space of categorical probability distributions. Instead, in this paper we explicitly leverage the information-geometric structure of the probability simplex by analyzing the dynamics of the process under the Fisher-Rao metric. We derive quantitative contraction and invariance bounds that are stable and do not become trivial as the dimensions increase. Thus, we show that the effective required data ratio to prevent model collapse is different than previously implied.

</details>


### [192] [NeuroECG: ECGFounder-Based Deep ECG Representation for EEG-Free Neurological Prognostication After Cardiac Arrest](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18891)
*Jiaju Gao,Yi Zhao,Chenyang Xu,Yuxi Zhou,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 NeuroECG，一种基于 ECGFounder 预训练模型的深度表征框架，利用床边单导联心电（ECG）在无脑电（EEG）条件下辅助预测心脏骤停后的神经功能预后。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心脏骤停后的神经预后判断通常依赖脑电图（EEG），但 EEG 对临床资源要求高；而床边心电（ECG）是标准且低成本的手段，其预测神经功能预后的价值却未被充分挖掘。

Method: 基于预训练 ECG 基础模型 ECGFounder 进行任务特定微调，采用渐进式解冻策略处理单通道床边监测 ECG；将每位患者的多段 ECG 编码为片段级深度特征，通过分位数池化（q=0.24）聚合，并用主成分分析（PCA）压缩，最后与静态临床协变量结合。

Result: 在多中心 I-CARE 数据库中 412 名有 ECG 数据的患者上，微调后的 ECGFounder 主干优于其他仅用 ECG 的主干基线，测试 AUROC 为 0.7333；NeuroECG 结合临床协变量后测试 AUROC 达 0.8077，AUPRC 为 0.8970。

Conclusion: 深度床边 ECG 表征可作为辅助预后信息的有效来源，与静态临床协变量结合后在无 EEG 场景下能提升预测性能。源代码已开源。

Abstract: Neurological prognostication after cardiac arrest commonly relies on electroencephalography (EEG). However, EEG demands high clinical resources. Bedside electrocardiography (ECG) is standard and low-cost. Yet, its value for predicting neurological outcomes remains underexplored. In this study, we propose NeuroECG, an ECGFounder-based deep representation framework for EEG-free auxiliary prognostication. NeuroECG adapts a pretrained ECG foundation model via task-specific fine-tuning. We implement a gradual unfreezing strategy on single-channel bedside monitoring ECG. Multiple ECG segments per patient are encoded into segment-level deep features. These embeddings are aggregated via quantile pooling ($q = 0.24$) and compressed using principal component analysis (PCA). Experiments on 412 ECG-available patients from the multicenter I-CARE database show that the adapted ECGFounder backbone achieves the best performance among ECG-only backbone baselines, with a test AUROC of 0.7333. We further combine the learned deep ECG representation with static clinical covariates. The proposed NeuroECG model achieves a test AUROC of 0.8077 and an AUPRC of 0.8970. These results support deep bedside ECG representations as a useful source of auxiliary prognostic information. Their integration with static clinical covariates improves prediction in an EEG-free setting. The source code is available at https://github.com/goddream66/NeuroECG_

</details>


### [193] [Higher-order pruning of experts in mixture-of-experts language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18916)
*Alex M. Tseng,Prannay Kaul,Luca Zancato,Wei Xia,Stefano Soatto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HOPE，一种基于二阶（高阶）目标函数的MoE专家剪枝方法，显式建模专家间的协作/交互效应，可将现有REAP视为忽略交互项的特例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE语言模型参数量巨大，造成显著的内存瓶颈；专家剪枝是最直接的减参方式，但现有方法对每个专家独立做剪枝决策，并假设专家贡献纯粹可加，忽略了MoE中专家使用本质上的协作性。

Method: 从理论上推导出HOPE（Higher-Order Pruning of Experts），即专家剪枝的二阶目标函数，可证明其最小化了剪枝所导致误差的一个上界；并证明最先进的一阶剪枝方法REAP是HOPE在忽略交互项时的特例。在三个前沿MoE模型（最大122B参数）、两个不同校准集及多个基准（数学、指令遵循、代码及agentic套件）上进行验证。

Result: HOPE的剪枝决策优于现有方法，优势在高剪枝率和困难agentic任务上尤为明显。50%剪枝率下，HOPE胜过所有基线，5种方法中平均排名1.58（次优的REAP为2.42），在agentic代码任务上最高提升+6.1%；在所有条件下HOPE平均排名最优，并在多数两两对比中胜出。

Conclusion: 通过保留一阶方法所忽略的专家协作结构，HOPE能够在最小性能损失下实现激进的压缩，尤其在需要长序列中多样专家组合的复杂任务上效果显著。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language models suffer from large parameter counts, which create a significant memory bottleneck. Expert pruning is the most direct approach for reducing this parameter count, yet existing methods make pruning decisions for each expert independently, and assume experts' contributions are purely additive. In reality, expert usage in MoEs is inherently cooperative. We derive HOPE (Higher-Order Pruning of Experts), a second-order pruning objective which provably minimizes an upper bound on the error resulting from pruning. We show that REAP (a state-of-the-art first-order pruning method) is a special case of HOPE where interaction terms are ignored. Across three frontier MoE models (up to 122B parameters), two distinct calibration sets, and multiple benchmarks (including math, instruction following, coding, and an agentic suite), we demonstrate that HOPE produces better pruning decisions than existing methods, and its advantage is most pronounced at high pruning rates and on challenging agentic workloads. At 50% pruning, HOPE outperforms all baselines and achieves an average rank of 1.58 out of 5 methods (versus 2.42 for the next-best method, REAP), with gains of up to +6.1% on agentic coding. Over all conditions, HOPE again achieves the best average rank and surpasses every other method in the majority of head-to-head comparisons. By preserving cooperative expert structure that first-order methods ignore, HOPE enables aggressive compression with minimal degradation, particularly on complex tasks where diverse expert combinations are invoked over long sequences.

</details>


### [194] [StableEval Arena: A Cost-Aware Agentic Benchmark for Stablecoin Price Stability Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18949)
*Sean Wan,Dongping Liu,Luyao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出StableEval Arena，一个成本感知的基准框架，用于评估Agentic AI系统在稳定币脱锚风险预测中的表现，涵盖预测质量、可靠性、延迟与推理成本，并发布了数据集与代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估多仅以准确率排名智能体，忽视可信度应同时包含预测质量、运行可靠性与计算成本；同时稳定币脱锚风险预测中，稀有严重压力事件难以捕捉，缺乏系统化的评测基准。

Method: 构建成本感知基准框架，采用防泄漏历史回放，结合交易所价量数据与市场情境特征，在隐藏的七天预测窗口上评估LLM驱动的Agentic系统；设置120例压力富集验证集与507例自然分布全集两个实验模块，对六种LLM智能体配置及基线，测量预测质量、校准标签行为、结构化输出可靠性、延迟、token消耗与估计推理成本。

Result: 智能体能够以较低成本稳定生成合法的结构化输出，但在稀有严重压力与持续脱锚案例上仍大多漏判；结果揭示协议遵循可靠性与金融风险可靠性之间存在明显差距。

Conclusion: 该框架将可信度视为预测质量、运行可靠性与计算成本的联合属性，而非单一准确率排名；为支持审计与复现，研究公开了基准数据集（Hugging Face）与源代码（GitHub）。

Abstract: We introduce StableEval Arena, a cost-aware benchmark framework for evaluating agentic AI systems on stablecoin peg-risk prediction. StableEval Arena evaluates LLM-backed agentic systems on diagnosing peg stress and forecasting deviations from the one-dollar peg over a hidden seven-day horizon, using leakage-safe historical replay with exchange price-volume data and market-context features. We report two complementary experiment blocks: a 120-case stress-enriched validation block and a 507-case natural-distribution full-arena evaluation block. Across six LLM-backed agent configurations and baselines, StableEval Arena measures prediction quality, calibrated-label behavior, structured-output reliability, latency, token consumption, and estimated inference cost. Rather than ranking agents by accuracy alone, the framework treats trustworthiness as a joint property of forecast quality, operational reliability, and computational cost. The results show a gap between protocol-following reliability and financial-risk reliability: agents reliably produce valid structured outputs at modest measured cost, but still miss most rare severe-stress and sustained-depeg cases. To support auditing and replication, we release the benchmark dataset on Hugging Face and the source code on GitHub.

</details>


### [195] [Changepoint-Aware World Models: Detecting Dynamics Shifts and Recovering by Forgetting Stale Replay in Model-Based RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18950)
*Everest Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CAWM，一种能检测动力学突变并清除陈旧回放数据的DreamerV3智能体，在机器人动力学突变下恢复更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习到的自身动力学模型在动力学变化（执行器磨损、负载变化、关节变硬）后失效，而模型智能体若继续照常训练，会被充满陈旧经验的回放缓冲拖累，适应缓慢。需要一个能感知动力学突变并相应调整的机制。

Method: Changepoint-Aware World Models (CAWM) 基于DreamerV3智能体，利用自身内部预测误差，通过在线CUSUM检验和滚动基线检测突发的动力学变化（只对突变触发，而非缓慢的学习漂移）；检测后清除陈旧的回放数据，保留已学到的表征但丢弃过时数据。

Result: 在模拟运动任务中，针对双倍重力和减半执行器增益两种突变，CAWM恢复速度显著快于被动重训练；也优于检测时重生成全新动力学模型的强基线。突变触发响应后，CAWM在三个随机种子上于突变后前30k帧获得+95到+153的回报增益，并在渐近性能上与该重生成基线持平；闭环运行检测器在重力突变上复现了该增益。

Conclusion: 该方法的收益在两种突变类型上均成立，且当突变严重到旧数据确实过时之时收益最大。

Abstract: A robot's learned model of its own dynamics is only valid until those dynamics change: actuators wear, payloads shift, and joints stiffen. A model-based agent that keeps training as if nothing happened adapts slowly, dragged back by a replay buffer full of stale experience. We present Changepoint-Aware World Models (CAWM), a DreamerV3 agent that detects an abrupt dynamics shift from its own internal prediction error, using an online CUSUM test against a rolling baseline that fires only on abrupt change rather than on slow learning drift. It then forgets stale replay, keeping the learned representation while flushing obsolete data. On simulated locomotion under two robot-relevant shifts, doubled gravity and halved actuator gain, CAWM recovers substantially faster than passive retraining. It also beats a strong baseline that respawns a fresh dynamics model on detection, the deep-world-model analogue of model-bank methods. With the response triggered at the shift, CAWM gains +95 to +153 return in the first 30k post-shift frames over three seeds, while matching that respawn at asymptote. Running the detector in closed loop reproduces this gain on the gravity shift. The benefit holds across both shift types, and is largest when the shift is severe enough that old data is genuinely obsolete.

</details>


### [196] [FedGuide: Diffusion Prior Alignment and Value Baseline Guidance for Heterogeneous Federated Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18964)
*Zhilin He,Gauri Joshi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 FedGuide，一种利用扩散先验作为行为模型的联邦强化学习框架，通过 OT-MoE 聚合扩散先验并用 DICE 值基线提供指导，缓解异构客户端间的分布失配，在多种异构环境下性能优于代表性 FRL 方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦强化学习方法（基于方差缩减、散度惩罚、动量优化）主要同步策略或价值网络参数，未显式处理异构客户端之间的分布失配问题，导致在强异构环境下学习不稳定、性能受限。

Method: FedGuide 使用扩散先验作为行为模型，为异构本地策略学习提供个性化的数据支撑分布；不直接平均本地策略，而是通过最优传输专家混合（OT-MoE）聚合扩散先验，在分布空间中保留异构行为模式；并引入分布校正估计（DICE）值基线，为本地策略改进提供低方差、回报感知的指导。

Result: 在多种异构环境实验中，FedGuide 在客户端平均回报、最终轮次性能和最差轮次鲁棒性上均优于代表性 FRL 方法，并在更强的异构条件下保持稳定的学习表现。

Conclusion: 通过将扩散先验与最优传输专家混合、DICE 值基线相结合，FedGuide 从分布层面而非单纯参数层面对齐异构客户端，为联邦强化学习在异构环境下的稳定与鲁棒学习提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Federated Reinforcement Learning (FRL) enables collaborative policy learning across distributed agents with heterogeneous environments. While recent methods based on variance reduction, divergence penalization, and momentum optimization improve FRL under heterogeneous settings, they still primarily synchronize policy or value-network parameters and do not explicitly address distributional mismatch among heterogeneous clients. Therefore, we propose \textbf{FedGuide}, a FRL framework that uses diffusion priors as behavior models to provide personalized data supported distributions for heterogeneous local policy learning. Instead of directly averaging local policies, FedGuide aggregates those diffusion priors through Optimal-Transport Mixture-of-Experts (OT-MoE), preserving heterogeneous behavior modes in distribution space. It further develops a Distribution Correction Estimation (DICE) value baseline to provide low-variance, return-aware guidance for local policy improvement. Experiments across heterogeneous environments show that FedGuide outperforms representative FRL methods in client-average returns, final-round performance, and worst-round robustness, while maintaining stable learning under stronger heterogeneity.

</details>


### [197] [The Automaton Underneath: The Additive Input Pathway Is a Parasitic Attractor for State Tracking in Householder Linear RNN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18966)
*Gunner Levi Howe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过预注册的因果消融实验证明，线性RNN（DeltaNet类）中加性输入注入项 b_t = W_b e_t 是导致长度泛化失败的"寄生"通路：将其置零后，模型能精确学习并泛化自动机，且所需Householder因子数等于任务生成元的最大反射长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作发现，具有输入依赖Householder乘积转移的线性RNN虽可证明能表示困难的状�态跟踪自动机，但训练后的模型无法进行长度泛化。近期研究将此归因于优化问题，却缺乏因果层面的解释。本文旨在提供这一因果解释。

Method: 采用预注册的、架构内因果消融方法：在相同模型基础上仅删除一项——加性输入注入 b_t = W_b e_t。在 parity、S_4、A_5、非可解 S_5 词问题等任务上测试含/不含该项的表现。同时提出并检验一个表示定律（每token的Householder因子最小数等于任务生成元在格式固定表示下的最大反射长度）。设置两个预注册实验臂定位机制：(i) 在已验证精确解处初始化且 W_b=0，观察Adam是否增长加性通路并拉离精确解；(ii) 检验"拟合路由经过 b_t"这一预测的击杀标准。共202次运行全部预注册。

Result: 含 b_t 时，模型仅拟合长度32，在分布外崩溃（S_5 位置512处准确率0.20）。不含 b_t 时，同一架构学到精确自动机：中位准确率1.00，达训练长度16倍，且在表示定律允许的各宽度均成立（parity 1，S_4 3，A_5与S_5 4）；低于该宽度则无法拟合。表示定律相对于表示形式：任务格式是状态跟踪基准中的隐藏变量。机制定位：(i) 在精确解处，Adam增长加性路径并将模型拉离精确解，而 -b 对照组保持1.00；(ii) 所有49个拟合种子在 W_b:=0 下仍保持域内拟合，且在定律最小宽度下，推理时置零 W_b 可恢复精确泛化（parity 5/5，S_5 5/5，S_4 4/5，A_5 4/5）。

Conclusion: 加性通路是寄生性的：它先使一个已正确学到的自动机失稳，随后又将其掩盖。所有202次运行均为预注册，所有数值均可从制品中复现。

Abstract: Linear RNNs with input-dependent Householder-product transitions (DeltaNet/DeltaProduct-class) can provably represent hard state-tracking automata, yet trained models fail to length-generalize -- a gap recent work attributes to optimization, without a causal account. We give one, in a pre-registered, within-architecture causal ablation: the same model with one term deleted -- the additive input injection $b_t = W_b e_t$. With $b_t$, models fit length 32 and collapse out-of-distribution on parity, $S_4$, $A_5$, and non-solvable $S_5$ word problems (0.20 at position 512 on $S_5$). Without it -- input acting only through the orthogonal transitions -- the same architecture learns the exact automaton: median accuracy 1.00 at 16x the training length, at every width admitted by a representation law we state and test: the minimal number of Householder factors per token equals the maximal reflection length of the task's generators in the format-pinned representation (parity 1, $S_4$ 3, $A_5$ and $S_5$ 4); below it, nothing fits. The contrast with DeltaProduct's $S_4$/$A_5$ at $n_h{=}2$ (group-element classification, $SO(3)$ realization) shows the law is representation-relative: task format is a hidden variable in state-tracking benchmarks. Two pre-registered arms locate the mechanism. (i) Initialized at a verified-exact solution with $W_b{=}0$, Adam grows the additive path and pulls the model off the exact solution; $-b$ controls stay at 1.00. (ii) Our registered prediction that the fit routes through $b_t$ fired its kill criterion: all 49 fitting seeds retain in-domain fit under $W_b{:=}0$ -- and at law-minimal width, zeroing $W_b$ at inference restores exact generalization (parity 5/5, $S_5$ 5/5, $S_4$ 4/5, $A_5$ 4/5). The additive pathway is parasitic: it destabilizes, then conceals, a correctly learned automaton. All 202 runs pre-registered; all numbers regenerate from artifacts.

</details>


### [198] [One Axis, No Brake: Self-Knowledge Limits the Filtering of Harmful Peer Conformity in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18998)
*Yibo Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 多智能体LLM系统的纠错机制受限于模型自识能力天花板（"墙"），在群体规模下会演变为错误共识放大的"悬崖"，有效的改进方向是修订前注入信息而非修订后过滤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统被认为更可靠，因为智能体能互相纠错；但同侪压力是双刃剑，既能修正错误答案也能推翻正确答案。作者希望构建一个"刹车"机制，保留有益修订、阻断有害修订。本文论证该刹车难以实现：因为一次修订是有害的，恰好等价于原始答案是正确的时候，所以是否阻断该修订，等价于判断模型原本是否已经正确。

Method: 将"寻找刹车"这一开放性问题转化为可测量的单一量——模型的自识能力（self-knowledge）。论证任何基于部署时信号的刹车本质上是伪装后的正确性探针；并在六个模型家族上测量自识能力（AUROC约0.64–0.89）。进一步尝试白盒引导模型自身的"正确性方向"，检验能否突破该上限。同时在"选择题社会"中做种群规模实验，考察智能体数量、模型多样性、更强成员等因素的影响。

Result: 自识能力远非完美，AUROC约为0.64–0.89（六个模型家族），构成难以突破的上限，作者称之为"墙"。即便用白盒方法引导模型的正确性方向也无法突破：它只改变模型修订的频率，而有害修订与有益修订同步变化。在种群尺度上，"墙"变成"悬崖"：当多数智能体初始答案错误时，辩论会把共同错误放大为自信的错误共识。增加智能体数量、提高模型多样性、加入更强成员都不能解决问题。

Conclusion: 该"刹车"机制在原理上难以构建，其上限由模型自识能力决定，且白盒干预无法逾越。群体规模下错误会被放大为自信的错误共识。有效做法是在修订之前注入信息，而非在修订之后进行过滤。局部一致并不等于全局正确。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems are expected to be more reliable because agents can catch each other's mistakes. But peer pressure cuts both ways: the same correction that fixes a wrong answer can overturn a right one. The tempting safeguard is a brake that keeps the beneficial revisions and blocks the harmful ones. We show this brake is hard to build, for a simple reason: a revision is harmful exactly when the original answer was right, so deciding whether to block it is the same as knowing whether the model was already correct. This turns the open-ended hunt for a brake into one measurable quantity, the model's self-knowledge: any brake built from a deploy-time signal is a correctness probe in disguise, and self-knowledge is far from perfect (AUROC $\approx 0.64$--$0.89$ across six model families). We call this ceiling the wall. Even white-box steering of the model's own correctness direction does not breach it: it changes how often the model revises, but harmful and beneficial revisions move together. At population scale the wall becomes the cliff: when most agents start wrong, debate amplifies the shared mistake into a confident, wrong consensus. In our multiple-choice societies, more agents, more model diversity, and a stronger member do not fix it. What helps is adding information before the revision, not filtering after it. Local agreement is not global correctness.

</details>


### [199] [Capability Emergence Can Be Forecast: Per-Seed, In Advance, With Calibrated Intervals, Certified False Alarms, and a Blind Pre-Registered Gate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19000)
*Gunner Levi Howe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将"涌现能力不可预测"的既有观点转化为可检验的预测问题，提出对涌现时间进行逐运行、提前、带校准不确定性的预测方法，并在grokking系统与小型语言模型上验证了前兆指标（如上一token head）能高精度领先预测induction head的涌现时机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前关于涌现能力早期预警指标的研究仅提供指标，而未将其作为"预测"来评分：缺乏受控假警报率下的领先时间、校准、负样本和盲测。作者希望为涌现时间预测建立严格的评估纪律。

Method: 在30个相同配置的transformer及小型语言模型中，以"上一token head"的形成时间预测各随机种子induction head的涌现时间；使用conformal区间给出校准不确定性，并设置冻结规则在从未见过的配置上进行预注册盲测；进一步设计带陷阱的语言阶梯攻击自身规则，用机制组合的合取条件区分假警报；最后通过gap-origin研究揭示乘法法则t_event≈1.19×t_anchor。

Result: 上一token head形成时间以Spearman rho=0.977预测涌现，中位领先975步（约训练15%）；最佳loss规则仅有50步领先（类似nowcast）。Conformal区间覆盖15/15留出种子，冻结规则在两个未见配置盲测通过（10/10和9/10）。陷阱阶梯中裸前兆在10/10能力受阻运行上假警报，而机制组合合取在两类语言中0假警报且rho=1.000。gap不固定（lr和batch均使其移动约2.3倍），80个有效锚点运行显示锚点在0.843的时间点触发，乘法规则在第三个未见配置盲测通过（5/5），假警报经33个制造负样本认证。前兆在3个公开模型家族（Pythia、OLMo、OLMo-2，7个套件）中领先，OLMo-2在1B token时前兆已形成而能力尚缺。

Conclusion: 涌现能力的时机在grokking系统和语言模型中是可提前、逐运行预测的，并可通过校准区间与盲测验证；前兆指标需与机制组合以避免假警报；涌现时间与锚点时间呈约1.19倍的乘法关系。作者报告了四条预注册kill criteria被触发，且所有freeze均先于数据出现在公开commit链中。

Abstract: Emergent capabilities are widely treated as unpredictable: loss improves smoothly while abilities appear abruptly. Prior work offers early-warning indicators but never scores them as forecasts: no lead time at controlled false-alarm rate, no calibration, no negatives, no blind tests. We supply that discipline and show that, in grokking model systems and small language models, emergence timing is forecastable per run, in advance, with calibrated uncertainty. Across 30 transformers at identical configuration, the formation time of the previous-token head forecasts each seed's induction-head emergence at Spearman rho=0.977 with median lead 975 steps (~15% of training); a best-case loss rule ties the ranking with 50-step lead (a nowcast). Conformal intervals covered 15/15 held-out seeds, and the frozen rule passed blind pre-registered gates on TWO never-seen configurations (10/10 and 9/10 coverage). A trap-language rung then attacked our own rule as pre-registered: where previous-token context pays for the task itself, the bare precursor false-alarms on 10/10 capability-blocked runs, while the mechanism-composed conjunction is certified in both language classes (0 false alarms) and times emergence at rho=1.000. Finally, a gap-origin study broke the fixed offset (both lr and batch move the gap ~2.3x; no external clock owns it) and revealed the law beneath: across 80 valid-anchor runs the anchor fires at 0.843 of time-to-emergence -- t_event ~= 1.19 x t_anchor -- and this multiplicative rule passed its own blind gate (5/5) at a third unseen configuration. False alarms are certified against 33 manufactured negatives. The precursor leads across 3 public model families (Pythia, OLMo, OLMo-2; 7 suites), with OLMo-2 at 1B tokens showing precursor formed, capability absent. Four pre-registered kill criteria fired and are reported. Every freeze precedes its data in a public commit chain.

</details>


### [200] [CompileRover: Revolutionizing Virtual Machine Compiler Optimization with a Tri-Role LLM-Driven Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19004)
*Mingqiao Mo,Yunlong Tan,Hao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 CompileRover 虚拟机编译器优化框架，采用裁判-顾问-操作员三角色协作机制，通过控制流分析、代码结构变换与动态执行模式识别，在多项基准上超越现有最优虚拟机编译器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有虚拟机编译器生成的代码常存在冗余计算、低效循环结构与次优函数实现，导致执行效率受损，亟需针对性的优化框架加以改进。

Method: 设计三角色协作机制（裁判、顾问、操作员）；综合运用控制流分析、代码结构变换、动态执行模式识别等优化算法与新方法。

Result: 在多种基准测试中持续超越当前最优虚拟机编译器，执行性能显著提升；优化有效降低执行开销、提升数据流一致性并稳健增强编译器性能。

Conclusion: CompileRover 是一种有效且可靠的虚拟机编译器优化方案，能突破性能瓶颈，具备较好的实用与推广价值。

Abstract: Code optimization plays a crucial role in the development of virtual machine compilers, with optimization frameworks significantly enhancing the performance of generated assembly code. However, existing virtual machine compiler outputs frequently exhibit redundant computations, inefficient loop structures, and suboptimal function implementations, which collectively impair execution efficiency. To address these shortcomings, we propose CompileRover, an advanced optimization framework specifically designed for virtual machine compilers. CompileRover employs a sophisticated three-role collaboration mechanism, comprising a referee, an advisor, and an operator, effectively overcoming performance bottlenecks by leveraging comprehensive optimization algorithms and novel methodologies, including control flow analysis, code structure transformations, and dynamic execution pattern recognition. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that CompileRover consistently surpasses state-of-the-art virtual machine compilers, achieving significant improvements in execution performance across various benchmarks. Furthermore, performance analyses validate that the introduced optimizations notably reduce execution overhead, improve dataflow consistency, and robustly enhance compiler performance, showcasing CompileRover as an effective and reliable approach to optimizing virtual machine compilers.

</details>


### [201] [Tabular Deep Learning vs Classical Machine Learning for Urban Land Cover Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19010)
*Muntasir Tabasum,Tanpia Tasnim,Md. Ekramul Islam,Al Zadid Sultan Bin Habib*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究基于UCI城市土地覆盖（ULC）数据集，在统一可复现的流程中对比经典机器学习模型与表格深度学习（TDL）模型，并针对类别不平衡采用加权交叉熵损失，发现树集成模型是强基线，而TDL模型在非线性交互显著且不平衡处理有效时可达到或超越其性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市土地覆盖分类对城市规划、环境监测和可持续发展至关重要。该任务面临高维度、异构特征和类别不平衡等遥感典型挑战，因此需要系统比较经典机器学习与表格深度学习方法的适用性与优劣。

Method: 使用UCI机器学习库的ULC数据集，该数据集包含源自高分辨率航空影像、覆盖九类地物（如道路、树木、草地、水体）的表格特征。在统一、可复现的流程中，将逻辑回归、SVM、随机森林、XGBoost、CatBoost等经典模型与TabNet、FT-Transformer、TabTransformer、TabSeq及1D CNN等TDL模型进行基准对比；采用加权交叉熵损失应对类别不平衡，并用准确率、宏精确率、宏召回率、宏F1、AUC-ROC和混淆矩阵评估性能。

Result: 树集成模型仍是较强的通用基线；当非线性交互显著且类别不平衡处理得当时，TDL模型能够达到或超过树集成模型的性能，为城市土地覆盖制图提供互补优势。

Conclusion: 经典机器学习与表格深度学习方法在城市土地覆盖分类中各有优势，TDL并非普遍优于树集成，其效果依赖非线性交互强度与不平衡处理策略，两类方法可互为补充。

Abstract: Urban Land Cover (ULC) classification plays a crucial role in urban planning, environmental monitoring, and sustainable development. We study this task using the ULC dataset from the UCI Machine Learning Repository, which includes tabular features derived from high-resolution aerial imagery across nine classes (e.g., roads, trees, grass, water). The dataset presents typical remote sensing challenges, including high dimensionality, heterogeneous features, and class imbalance. In a unified, reproducible pipeline, we benchmark classical machine learning models (e.g., Logistic Regression, SVM, Random Forest, XGBoost, CatBoost) against Tabular Deep Learning (TDL) models (TabNet, FT-Transformer, TabTransformer, TabSeq, and 1D CNNs). To address class imbalance, we employ weighted cross-entropy loss for TDL models and evaluate performance using accuracy, macro-precision, macro-recall, macro-F1, AUC-ROC, and confusion matrices. Our results show that while tree ensembles remain strong general baselines, TDL models can match or exceed their performance when non-linear interactions are significant and imbalance handling is effective, providing complementary advantages for urban land cover mapping. See code: https://github.com/mtesha/tdl-vs-ml-urbanlandcover

</details>


### [202] [TwinMark: A Unified Watermark for Provable Survival Under Feature and Logit Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19011)
*Redwanul Karim,Tobias Feigl,Christopher Mutschler,Felix Ott*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TwinMark 是一种通过两个互补线性泛函读取同一 SHAKE128 密钥的水印方案，在知识蒸馏攻击下具有可测的后验认证，并在多数据集、多架构、多任务上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已部署视觉模型面临知识蒸馏（KD）等提取攻击，现有水印方案难以在不同暴露面（分类器 API 与仅表征主机）上同时提供可认证的检测能力。

Method: 提出 TwinMark，用两个互补线性泛函读取单一 SHAKE128 密钥：cov-Feat（针对载体集协方差的协方差投影器）与 cc-FALC（从类均值 logits 解码的类条件 Fisher 对齐线性载体）。两路共享同一比特向量，分别覆盖分类器 API（KL-KD）与仅表征主机（FM-KD）两种提取面；每路提供教师可测的后验认证以给出蒸馏后检测功效下界，并在限制性 OR 规则下组合。

Result: cov-Feat 具秩盲算子范数认证，cc-FALC 的居中 logit 间隙认证使比特容量与类别数解耦：在 K=1024、m=100 类（10.24 倍过编码）下比特投票达 z=23.0σ，教师精度代价仅 +0.9±0.2%p。在 CIFAR-10/100 与 Mini-ImageNet 的 13 种攻击中，凡攻击后模型保留任务效用的单元均验证成功；可跨架构蒸馏至 ResNet-18/50、VGG-16、MobileNet-V3，并迁移到 GNSS 少样本、VOC 检测、ISIC 分割与 STL-10 SimCLR。

Conclusion: TwinMark 通过双互补读出与可认证检测下界，在多种蒸馏攻击与跨架构、跨任务场景下实现了鲁棒且高容量的模型水印。

Abstract: We propose TwinMark, a watermarking scheme that reads a single SHAKE128 secret through two complementary linear functionals of model-output summaries: a covariance projector against the carrier-set covariance (cov-Feat) and a class-conditional Fisher-aligned linear carrier decoded from class-mean logits (cc-FALC). The two readouts share one bit vector and cover the two extraction surfaces of a deployed vision model: a classifier API attacked by KL knowledge distillation (KD) (Std. KL-KD), and a representation-only host attacked by feature-matching KD (FM-KD). Each readout admits a teacher-measurable a posteriori certificate that lower-bounds post-distillation detection power, and the two channels combine under a regime-restricted OR rule whose test statistic (calibrated null or bit vote) is selected by the exposed surface. cov-Feat admits a rank-blind operator-norm certificate, cc-FALC admits a centered-logit-gap certificate that decouples bit capacity from class count: at K=1024 in m=100 classes (a 10.24x over-encoding), the bit-vote attains z=23.0 sigma at a teacher-accuracy cost of +0.9+-0.2%p. Across 13 attacks on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and Mini-ImageNet, TwinMark verifies on every cell whose post-attack model retains task utility, survives cross-architecture distillation onto ResNet-18/50, VGG-16, and MobileNet-V3, and ports to GNSS few-shot, VOC detection, ISIC segmentation, and STL-10 SimCLR.

</details>


### [203] [Integrated Optimization of Automated Warehouse Operations and Last-Mile Transport for Differentiated On-Demand Delivery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19048)
*Xiaozhu Sun,Bilal Farooq*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种AGV智能仓储与末端多式联运的集成优化方法，基于深度强化学习实现两系统动态联动，实验表明在准时交付、配送时间和距离等方面显著优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在差异化按需货物配送场景下，仓储作业与末端运输相互割裂，难以满足高吞吐连续订单调度、竞争性需求以及系统敏感性与适应性的要求，因此需要一种集成优化方法来打通两个系统。

Method: 设计面向多目标联合调度的深度强化学习算法；仓储端提出基于多目标、多奖励机制、机器感知A*引导深度Q网络的改进算法（MORM-AGDQN），融合服务水平、系统成本与外部运输需求；外部运输端提出基于多奖励、多头注意力异质容量车辆路径问题的改进算法（MRMH-HCVRP），结合优化后的调度订单序列与分组，以及客户位置、需求、优先级、车辆容量、速度与服务范围。

Result: 所提框架显著优于传统方法：仓储作业准时交付率达100%；联合优化后末端平均配送时间降低29.3%至53.2%，总运输距离减少46.4%，高优先级服务率提升至92%以上，并在运营成本与客户满意度之间保持平衡。

Conclusion: 通过深度强化学习实现仓储与末端运输的动态集成优化，可在提升服务质量和效率的同时兼顾成本与客户满意度。

Abstract: In the context of differentiated on-demand goods delivery services, this study proposes an integrated optimization method for automated guided vehicles (AGVs) based smart warehouse operations and the last-mile multi-modal transport. A deep reinforcement learning algorithm for multi-objective joint scheduling is designed to establish a dynamic connection between two systems, solving key challenges such as achieving high-throughput continuous order scheduling, meeting competing requirements, and improving the overall system sensitivity and adaptability. For warehouse optimization within this framework, we propose an improved algorithm based on multi-objective, Multi-Reward Machines-A* Guided Deep Q-Network (MORM-AGDQN), which combines service level, system cost, and external transportation demand. For external optimization, we propose an improved algorithm based on a Multi-Reward, Multi Head attention-Heterogeneous Capacity Vehicle Routing Problem (MRMH-HCVRP) framework, which incorporates the optimized scheduling order sequence and grouping, combined with customer location, demand, and priority, vehicle capacity, speed, and service range. The results show that the proposed framework significantly outperforms traditional methods, achieving 100% on-time delivery rate for warehousing operations. After joint optimization, the average delivery time for the last mile was reduced by 29.3% to 53.2%, the total transportation distance was reduced by 46.4%, the high-priority service rate was increased to over 92%, and a balance was maintained between operating costs and customer satisfaction.

</details>


### [204] [LightSleepX: A Lightweight, Inception-Based Dual-Modal Network for Sleep Staging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19062)
*Yi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LightSleepX：一个轻量级睡眠分期框架，在资源受限环境下实现鲁棒睡眠分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动睡眠分期对个人健康监测至关重要，但现有方法难以适用于实际场景：传统流程依赖手工特征或浅层模型，泛化能力差；而先进的深度学习虽精度高，却计算量大，难以在资源受限环境中部署。因此需要一个兼顾精度与轻量化的方案。

Method: 提出LightSleepX框架：采用Inception风格架构结合深度可分离卷积与多尺度增强注意力（Multi-scale Enhanced Attention），实现高效的多模态EEG/EOG特征提取；并使用Mamba编码器进行无规则的长程时序建模。

Result: 在公开基准数据集上，Sleep-EDF-20达到85.9%准确率和0.803的macro-F1；在跨被试ISRUC-S3数据集上达到81.8%准确率和0.796的macro-F1。模型参数量仅0.049M，计算量为195.9 MFLOPs。

Conclusion: LightSleepX以极低的计算成本实现良好的睡眠分期性能，面向计算成本与隐私为核心约束的本地部署场景，适合实际应用。

Abstract: Automatic sleep staging is fundamental to personal health monitoring, yet many existing approaches are ill-suited for real-world applications. Traditional pipelines often rely on hand-crafted features or shallow machine learning models that struggle to generalize, while state-of-the-art deep learning methods, though accurate, are computationally heavy and impractical for resource-constrained environments. This paper introduces LightSleepX, a lightweight framework designed to deliver robust sleep analysis in resource-constrained environments. LightSleepX combines an Inception-style architecture with depthwise separable convolutions and Multi-scale Enhanced Attention for efficient multi-modal EEG/EOG feature extraction, and a Mamba encoder for rule-free long-range temporal modeling. On public benchmark datasets, LightSleepX achieves 85.9% accuracy and a 0.803 macro-F1 score on Sleep-EDF-20, and 81.8% accuracy and a 0.796 macro-F1 score on the cross-subject ISRUC-S3 dataset. With 0.049M parameters and 195.9 MFLOPs, the framework targets practical local deployment where computational cost and privacy are central constraints.

</details>


### [205] [RLLBC-Lib: An Educational Code Library for Reinforcement Learning and Learning-Based Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19074)
*Bernd Frauenknecht,Emma Cramer,Artur Eisele,Paul Kruse,Lukas Kesper,Jonas Hertrampf,Ramil Sabirov,Jyotirmaya Patra,Johannes Berger,Paul Brunzema,Friedrich Solowjow,Sebastian Trimpe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了 RLLBC-Lib，一个面向学习型控制场景的强化学习代码库，旨在降低学生学习 RL 的门槛，涵盖表格型 RL、深度 RL 及与其他学习型控制方法的对比，并支持自动评分作业。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习涉及多个对象间跨多周期的复杂交互，这些动态过程通常需要易于上手的实现才能被清晰解释。因此需要一个精心设计的代码库来降低学生和其他学习者在学习型控制背景下入门 RL 的障碍。

Method: RLLBC-Lib 包含三部分：(1) 一个全面的表格型 RL 方法库，以强化对理论基础的清晰理解；(2) 一个遵循相同设计原则的深度 RL 库，以凸显简单表格方法与前沿深度方法之间的平行关系；(3) 一批实现示例，用于说明核心 RL 原理并将 RL 与其他学习型控制方法进行对比。

Result: 该库成功将表格型与深度 RL 方法在统一设计原则下整合，并提供了核心原理示例及与学习型控制方法的对比实现，同时为构建带自动评分的编程作业提供了理想基础。

Conclusion: RLLBC-Lib 是一个精心设计的教学型 RL 代码库，通过统一表格与深度方法的设计、核心原理演示及自动评分支持，有效降低了学习型控制场景下 RL 的学习门槛。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) is an exciting concept as well as a remarkable success story worth sharing. However, RL builds on rather complex interactions between different objects that play out over several cycles. Such dynamics are often best explained with an easily accessible implementation. We present RLLBC-Lib, a carefully crafted code library with the goal of lowering the entry barrier for students and other learners of RL in the context of learning-based control. At its heart, RLLBC-Lib comprises a comprehensive library of tabular RL approaches to enforce a clear understanding of the theoretical foundations. A deep RL library follows the same design principles, underscoring the parallels between simple tabular and state-of-the-art deep RL approaches. Additionally, RLLBC-Lib provides a collection of implementations illustrating core RL principles and contrasting RL to other learning-based control approaches. Finally, RLLBC-Lib provides an ideal basis for creating programming assignments with automated grading.

</details>


### [206] [Double descent is the principle of least action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19076)
*Congzhou M Sha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文用统计力学解释双下降现象：将随机梯度法的训练轨迹视为在训练损失能量景观上以温度T扩散的粒子，有限训练时间带来有效权重衰减，使每个参数成为二次自由度。由能量均分定理，能量以T/2分配到d个自由度上，因此在固定训练损失下增加参数会降低温度，使玻尔兹曼分布趋于平稳路径；同时增加参数只会降低平稳路径的L2范数，等效增强权重正则化，从而解释测试误差先降、峰值、再降的双下降曲线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型的测试误差随参数数量d先下降、在模型刚好能拟合训练数据时达到峰值、随后再次下降，这一"双下降"现象已被观察到但缺乏理论解释。作者希望从统计力学角度给出机理解释。

Method: 把基于随机梯度的训练轨迹建模为在训练损失能量景观上以诱导温度T游走的粒子；假设已平衡的运行等概率访问给定训练损失的每个参数向量（统计力学基本假设），概率由玻尔兹曼分布给出。由于训练从初始点出发且扩散时间有限，训练携带有效权重衰减，使每个参数成为二次自由度。运用能量均分定理，将能量以T/2份额分配到d个自由度上。

Result: 在固定训练损失下，增加参数会降低温度，驱使玻尔兹曼分布趋向平稳路径；并且增加参数只会降低平稳路径的L2范数，因此在固定损失下采样的解随d增大而不太可能变大，从而有效增强权重正则化。

Conclusion: 双下降现象可由统计力学框架统一解释：有限训练时间下的有效权重衰减与能量均分共同作用，使得增加参数等价于降低温度并增强正则化，进而产生测试误差的双下降曲线。

Abstract: The test error of a model plotted against its number of parameters $d$ falls, peaks when the model can just fit the training data, and falls again, exhibiting the double descent phenomenon. We explain the phenomenon with statistical mechanics. The training trajectory of a stochastic gradient-based method is a particle wandering over the energy landscape of the training loss at an induced temperature $T$, and a run that has equilibrated visits every parameter vector of a given training loss equally often, the fundamental postulate of statistical mechanics, with probability given by the Boltzmann distribution. Because training starts at an initial point and has only finite time to diffuse, it carries an effective weight decay, which makes every parameter a quadratic degree of freedom. The equipartition theorem then distributes the energy among the $d$ degrees of freedom in shares of $T/2$, so at a fixed training loss adding parameters lowers the temperature and drives the Boltzmann distribution toward the stationary path. Finally, adding parameters can only lower the $L^2$ norm of the stationary path, so a solution sampled at fixed loss is less likely to be large with increasing $d$, effectively increasing weight regularization.

</details>


### [207] [Probabilistic Linear Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19077)
*Frederic Koriche,Jean-Marie Lagniez,Chi Tran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于稀疏锚定线性模型的统一概率可解释性框架，适用于二分类与连续回归，并通过MIP与IHT两种方法求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 溯因解释往往涉及过多特征而超出人类认知极限，且概率松弛方法此前主要局限于分类任务，缺乏对回归和一般模型的统一处理。

Method: 将实例映射到布尔超立方体，构建兼具大小与方向的稀疏锚定线性解释（稀疏预算k），刻画相关性误差在神经网络下的ClassNPPP-hard性，并将其与可处理的保真度误差建立上界关系（局部乘性因子较小）。采用两种方法求解：保证最优且多项式样本复杂度的MIP，以及具近似保证的多项式时间IHT算法。

Result: 实验表明，相比LIME、MAPLE等先进基线，该方法构造上即满足锚定与稀疏约束，且持续获得更低的相关性误差。

Conclusion: 该框架统一并推广了基于子集的可解释性方法，在分类与回归中同时保证稀疏性与锚定性，兼具理论保证与实证优势。

Abstract: Formal explainability provides mathematically grounded justifications for individual predictions. However, abductive explanations often exceed human cognitive limits by involving too many features, while probabilistic relaxations have remained largely limited to categorical classification. We present a unified framework for probabilistic explainability based on sparse, anchored linear models, applicable to both binary classification and continuous regression. By mapping instances to the Boolean hypercube, our linear explanations strictly generalize subset-based approaches: they capture both the magnitude and direction of feature contributions while enforcing a prescribed sparsity budget $k$. We show that minimizing the relevance error for such explanations is \ClassNPPP-hard when the underlying model is a neural network, and we relate this intractable objective to a tractable surrogate---the fidelity error. For a parameterized family of local distributions, the relevance error of any $k$-sparse explanation is bounded by its fidelity error up to a multiplicative factor that remains small locally. We address the resulting empirical problem using two complementary approaches: a Mixed Integer Programming (MIP) formulation that yields provably optimal empirical solutions while maintaining polynomial sample complexity, and a polynomial-time Iterative Hard Thresholding (IHT) algorithm with provable approximation guarantees. Empirical evaluations show that, unlike state-of-the-art baselines such as LIME and MAPLE, our explanations satisfy both the anchoring and sparsity constraints by construction, while consistently achieving lower relevance error.

</details>


### [208] [Evidence-Grounded Agentic Formulation Development in an Autonomous Laboratory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19099)
*Michael M. Craig,Riley J. Hickman,Yingshan Ma,Rémi Piché-Taillefer,Christine Allen,Pauric Bannigan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出智能体系统 Andromeda 2，通过整合内部结构化实验证据并调用计算与实验工具迭代设计自乳化给药系统配方，在紫杉醇配方筛选中显著优于概率优化模型与湿实验 DoE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自乳化给药系统（SEDDS）可提升难溶性药物的口服生物利用度，但高性能配方的筛选高度依赖大量实验，成本高、周期长，亟需更高效的配方设计方法。

Method: 构建 Andromeda 2 智能体系统，基于内部结构化实验证据进行推理，并调用计算与实验工具，在小型自动化实验室中于同等预算下连续设计并执行多批配方实验；与已部署于多个在研项目的概率优化模型 Andromeda 1 及湿实验 DoE 方案进行基准对比，并开展消融实验。

Result: 在紫杉醇上，Andromeda 2 的高性能命中率为 50%，Andromeda 1 为 17%，DoE 为 2%；满足全部四项 TPP 目标的配方数分别为 12、6、0；$AUC_{10-240}$ 中位数分别为 70.1、12.0、3.5 mg·min/mL，Andromeda 2 与 Andromeda 1 的最大 AUC 相当。优选的全 TPP 配方在首次 FaSSIF 测定中实现 19±5% w/w 的表观有效紫杉醇载药量，约为文献报道 5.7% w/w 的 3.3 倍。消融实验显示，获取内部结构化实验证据可使平均 AUC 提升 34%。

Conclusion: Andromeda 2 能有效利用结构化内部实验证据并自主调用计算与实验工具，在同等预算下大幅提升 SEDDS 配方筛选效率与性能，优于传统概率优化模型和常规 DoE 方案，为制剂开发提供了高效的智能化路径。

Abstract: Self-emulsifying drug delivery systems (SEDDS) can improve the oral bioavailability of poorly soluble drugs, but identifying high-performing formulations remains experimentally intensive. We present Andromeda 2, an agentic system that reasons over structured in-house experimental evidence and invokes computational and experimental tools to design and execute successive formulation batches. Using a miniaturized automated laboratory at a matched budget, we benchmark it against Andromeda 1, a probabilistic optimization model deployed across dozens of live development projects, and a wet-lab design-of-experiments (DoE) campaign. For paclitaxel, Andromeda 2 achieved a 50% high-performance hit rate versus 17% for Andromeda 1 and 2% for DoE, and identified 12 formulations meeting all four target product profile (TPP) objectives versus 6 and 0, respectively. Median $AUC_{10-240}$ was 70.1, 12.0, and 3.5 mg$\cdot$min/mL, while maximum AUC was comparable between Andromeda 2 and Andromeda 1. A selected full-TPP formulation achieved an apparent effective paclitaxel loading of $19 \pm 5\%$ w/w at the first FaSSIF measurement, approximately 3.3-fold higher than the 5.7% w/w loading reported for a published paclitaxel S-SEDDS. A controlled ablation showed that access to structured in-house experimental evidence increased mean AUC by 34%.

</details>


### [209] [How Model Growth, Recursion, and Boundary Operators Influence Scaling Exponents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19107)
*Zixi Chen,Akshay Vegesna,Samip Dahal,Andrew Gordon Wilson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出架构干预（如循环Transformer与模型增长）能改变预训练的缩放指数，从而在计算量增加时带来指数级性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观念认为缩放定律中架构无法改变缩放指数。作者希望探究是否可以通过架构设计（如循环深度、模型增长）来修改缩放指数，从而获得随规模递增的计算效率增益。

Method: 以循环Transformer（递归深度）为切入点，通过训练中增加循环次数实现模型增长（含共享权重与不共享权重）；此外在普通Transformer中加入边界算子（归一化并注入早期模块）；并在数据受限、多轮训练的设置下考察循环的正则化效应。

Result: 模型增长（无论是否共享权重）对缩放指数改变最大：7.4B模型增长架构在CORE上以约20倍更少算力匹敌GPT-3 13B，且效率增益随规模增大。边界算子也能带来递增的计算效率增益，但幅度较小。多轮训练中标准循环具有正则化作用，按规模增加循环数是计算最优的。

Conclusion: 结果可从计算深度视角理解：在给定算力预算下，增加Transformer的有效可用深度可带来随规模递增的效率增益，说明架构干预能突破传统缩放定律的限制。

Abstract: Scaling laws predict how loss decreases with increases in computation. We show, contrary to conventional wisdom, that architectural interventions can modify scaling exponents in pre-training, leading to exponential improvements in performance with increases in computation. As an anchoring point, we consider the architectural formulation of looped transformers. Although not typically used in this way, looping, also known as recursive depth, provides a mechanism for model growth, by increasing the number of loops during training. Model growth, with and without shared weights, provides the biggest changes to the scaling exponents. In particular, a 7.4B model growth architecture matches GPT-3 13B on CORE with roughly $20\times$ less compute, and has compute efficiency gains that increase with scale. Moreover, simply using a boundary operator in a vanilla transformer, which normalizes and injects an earlier block, also provides increasing compute-efficiency gains, although to a lesser extent. In the data-constrained, multi-epoch setting, standard looping has a useful regularizing effect, where we find it is compute-optimal to increase the number of loops with scale. These results can be understood through the lens of computational depth: for a given computational budget, we wish to increase the usable depth of the transformer, which can lead to efficiency gains that increase with scale.

</details>


### [210] [Exponential Hardness of Off-Policy Evaluation under History-Dependent Logging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19135)
*Pranaya Jajoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明：当日志记录依赖于历史时，即使数据覆盖充分，评估目标策略价值仍可能指数级不可行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答 Zhang 和 Jiang (2025) 提出的历史依赖日志、基于模型的策略评估问题，探究覆盖性条件是否足以保证有效评估。

Method: 对每个 H≥3，构造两个 POMDP，每阶段至多两个隐状态、三个动作，共享一个三记忆状态的日志器；分析其精确统计实验并给出匹配的最优估计器，同时用有向双通道网格世界实现。

Result: 动作覆盖、信念覆盖及两个行为边缘结果揭示条件均有独立于 H 的常数，但评估已知确定性目标策略至精度 1/8 需 Θ((3/2)^H log(1/δ)) 条日志轨迹；模拟与有限样本预测一致。

Conclusion: 在历史依赖日志的基于模型情形下，覆盖性不足以带来可处理性，该问题在行为边缘揭示定义下是指数级棘手的。

Abstract: Can a logged dataset visit every hidden state frequently and still be exponentially uninformative about a target policy's value? We show that it can when the logger depends on history. For every horizon $H \ge 3$, we construct two POMDPs with at most two latent states per stage, three actions, and a common logger with three memory states. Action coverage, belief coverage, and two behavior-marginal outcome-revealing conditions all have constants independent of $H$. Nevertheless, evaluating a known deterministic target policy to accuracy $1/8$ requires $Θ((3/2)^H \log(1/δ))$ logged episodes at confidence $1-δ$, for $0 < δ\le 1/4$, even when both candidate models are known. The mechanism is simple: a reset erases the unknown transition that determines the target value. We characterize the resulting statistical experiment exactly and obtain a matching optimal estimator. A directed two-lane gridworld realizes the construction, and trajectory simulations agree with its finite-sample prediction. The result establishes intractability for the history-dependent-logging, model-based case posed by Zhang and Jiang (2025, arXiv:2503.01134), under their behavior-marginal definition of revealing.

</details>
